    <!-- Modal Notifications -->
    <div id="notificationsModal" class="modal">
        <div class="modal-content" style="max-width: 450px;">
            <div class="modal-header"><h3><i class="fas fa-bell"></i> Notifications</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeNotificationsModal()">&times;</span></div>

    <script>
        let currentPage = 1;
        let totalPages = 1;
        let currentPageTraitement = 1;
        let totalPagesTraitement = 1;
        let traitementCourriers = [];
        let selectedTraitementIds = [];
        let currentTraitementFilter = 'all';
        let charts = {};
        let userPermissions = {};
        let userRole = '';
        let userService = '';
        let allCourriers = [];
        let pendingCourriers = [];
        let servicesUsers = {};
        let currentAssignCourrier = null;
        let currentTraiterAssignation = null;

        async function init() {
            await loadUserInfo();
            await loadUserPermissions();
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
            await loadAdvancedAnalytics();
            await loadAuditLogs();
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            initTabs();
            initCharts();
            setInterval(() => { loadNotifications(); }, 30000);
        }

        async function loadUserInfo() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/check_auth', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success && data.user) {
                    document.getElementById('username').textContent = data.user.nom_complet || data.user.username;
                    document.getElementById('userRole').textContent = data.user.role || 'Utilisateur';
                    document.getElementById('userService').textContent = data.user.service || '';
                    userRole = data.user.role;
                    userService = data.user.service;
                    document.getElementById('userRole').className = `user-role role-${data.user.role}`;
                } else { window.location.href = '/'; }
            } catch (error) { window.location.href = '/'; }
        }

        async function loadUserPermissions() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/user/permissions', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    userPermissions = data.permissions;
                    if (!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) document.getElementById('btnAddCourrier').style.display = 'none';
                    if (data.role !== 'directeur') {
                        document.getElementById('menuAudit').style.display = 'none';
                        document.getElementById('menuAssignation').style.display = 'block';
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('menuAssignation').style.display = 'block';
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur:', error); }
        }

        async function loadCourriers() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '';
            const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const priority = document.getElementById('priorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
            
            let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPage}&per_page=10`;
            if (search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
            if (status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
            if (priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayCourriersTable(allCourriers);
                    displayRecentCourriers(allCourriers);
                    updateStatsCards(allCourriers);
                    updateCharts(allCourriers);
                    totalPages = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfo').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = currentPage <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtn').disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
                    document.getElementById('courrierBadge').textContent = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur:', error); }
        }

        function displayCourriersTable(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('courriersTableBody');
            if (!courriers || courriers.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center">📭 Aucun courrier trouvé</span></span></td></span>';
                return;
            }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${c.id}</span></span></td>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 50))}${c.objet.length > 50 ? '...' : ''}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>
                        <button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})" title="Voir">👁️</button>
                        ${userPermissions.can_edit_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="editCourrier(${c.id})" title="Modifier">✏️</button>` : ''}
                        ${userPermissions.can_delete_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-delete" onclick="deleteCourrier(${c.id})" title="Supprimer">🗑️</button>` : ''}
                        ${c.scan_image_path ? `<button class="action-btn btn-scan" onclick="viewScan(${c.id})" title="Voir scan">📄</button>` : ''}
                    </span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        function displayRecentCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recentCourriers');
            if (!tbody) return;
            const recent = [...courriers].slice(-5).reverse();
            tbody.innerHTML = recent.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 40))}</td>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span></td>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span></td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        function updateStatsCards(courriers) {
            document.getElementById('statTotal').textContent = courriers.length;
            document.getElementById('statEnAttente').textContent = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            document.getElementById('statTraites').textContent = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            document.getElementById('statUrgents').textContent = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent' || c.priorite === 'high').length;
            // Mettre à jour le badge du traitement
            const enAttenteUrgent = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
            document.getElementById('traitementBadge').textContent = enAttenteUrgent;
        }

        // ==================== FONCTIONS TRAITEMENT ====================
        async function loadTraitementCourriers() {
            try {
                let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPageTraitement}&per_page=10`;
                if (currentTraitementFilter !== 'all') url += `&status=${currentTraitementFilter}`;
                const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    traitementCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayTraitementCourriers(traitementCourriers);
                    totalPagesTraitement = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfoTraitement').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPageTraitement} / ${totalPagesTraitement}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement >= totalPagesTraitement;
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur:', error); }
        }

        function displayTraitementCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('traitementCourriersTable');
            if (!courriers || courriers.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center">📭 Aucun courrier trouvé</span></span></td></span>';
                return;
            }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="traitement-checkbox" value="${c.id}" ${c.statut === 'traite' ? 'disabled' : ''} onchange="updateTraitementSelectedCount()"></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.id}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 50))}${c.objet.length > 50 ? '...' : ''}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        function updateTraitementSelectedCount() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:checked:not([disabled])');
            selectedTraitementIds = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
            const count = selectedTraitementIds.length;
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ ${count} courrier(s) sélectionné(s)`;
            
            const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            if (selectAllCheckbox && allCheckboxes.length > 0) {
                selectAllCheckbox.checked = allCheckboxes.length === checkboxes.length;
            }
        }

        function toggleSelectAllTraitement() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            const allChecked = Array.from(checkboxes).every(cb => cb.checked);
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = !allChecked);
            updateTraitementSelectedCount();
        }

        function toggleSelectAllFromCheckboxTraitement() {
            const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = selectAllCheckbox.checked);
            updateTraitementSelectedCount();
        }

        function filterTraitementCourriers(filter) {
            currentTraitementFilter = filter;
            currentPageTraitement = 1;
            document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(tab => tab.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelector(`.status-tab[data-filter="${filter}"]`).classList.add('active');
            loadTraitementCourriers();
        }

        function openMultipleTraiterModal() {
            if (selectedTraitementIds.length === 0) {
                showNotification('Veuillez sélectionner au moins un courrier à traiter', 'error');
                return;
            }
            const selectedCourriers = traitementCourriers.filter(c => selectedTraitementIds.includes(c.id));
            const listHtml = selectedCourriers.map(c => `
                <div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; font-size: 13px;">
                    <strong>#${c.id}</strong> - ${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 60))}
                    <span style="float: right;">${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)} ${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span>
                </div>
            `).join('');
            document.getElementById('selectedTraiterList').innerHTML = `<strong>${selectedCourriers.length} courrier(s) à traiter :</strong>${listHtml}`;
            document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value = '';
            document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmMultipleTraiter() {
            const commentaire = document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value;
            if (!commentaire) {
                showNotification('Veuillez ajouter un rapport de traitement', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (selectedTraitementIds.length === 0) {
                showNotification('Aucun courrier sélectionné', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            showNotification(`Traitement de ${selectedTraitementIds.length} courrier(s) en cours...`, 'info');
            
            let successCount = 0;
            let errorCount = 0;
            
            for (const courrierId of selectedTraitementIds) {
                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`/api/courriers/${courrierId}`, {
                        method: 'PUT',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        credentials: 'include',
                        body: JSON.stringify({ statut: 'traite' })
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (data.success) successCount++;
                    else errorCount++;
                } catch (error) { errorCount++; }
            }
            
            showNotification(`✅ ${successCount} courriers traités, ❌ ${errorCount} erreurs`, successCount > 0 ? 'success' : 'error');
            
            closeMultipleTraiterModal();
            selectedTraitementIds = [];
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ 0 courrier(s) sélectionné(s)`;
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
        }

        function previousPageTraitement() { if (currentPageTraitement > 1) { currentPageTraitement--; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }
        function nextPageTraitement() { if (currentPageTraitement < totalPagesTraitement) { currentPageTraitement++; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== FIN FONCTIONS TRAITEMENT ====================

        function initCharts() {
            const ctxStatus = document.getElementById('statusChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPriority = document.getElementById('priorityChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxMonthly = document.getElementById('monthlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxTop = document.getElementById('topExpediteursChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxService = document.getElementById('serviceChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPerformance = document.getElementById('performanceChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxHourly = document.getElementById('hourlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxWeekly = document.getElementById('weeklyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxProcessing = document.getElementById('processingTimeChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxML = document.getElementById('mlPredictionChart')?.getContext('2d');
            
            if (ctxStatus) charts.statusChart = new Chart(ctxStatus, { type: 'doughnut', data: { labels: ['En attente', 'Traité', 'Urgent', 'Archivé'], datasets: [{ data: [0,0,0,0], backgroundColor: ['#ffc107','#28a745','#dc3545','#17a2b8'] }] } });
            if (ctxPriority) charts.priorityChart = new Chart(ctxPriority, { type: 'pie', data: { labels: ['Haute', 'Moyenne', 'Basse'], datasets: [{ data: [0,0,0], backgroundColor: ['#dc3545','#ffc107','#28a745'] }] } });
            if (ctxMonthly) charts.monthlyChart = new Chart(ctxMonthly, { type: 'line', data: { labels: [], datasets: [{ label: 'Courriers reçus', data: [], borderColor: '#1e3c72', tension: 0.3 }] } });
            if (ctxTop) charts.topExpediteursChart = new Chart(ctxTop, { type: 'bar', data: { labels: [], datasets: [{ data: [], backgroundColor: '#2a5298' }] } });
            if (ctxService) charts.serviceChart = new Chart(ctxService, { type: 'doughnut', data: { labels: [], datasets: [{ data: [], backgroundColor: ['#1e3c72','#2a5298','#28a745','#17a2b8','#6c757d'] }] } });
            if (ctxPerformance) charts.performanceChart = new Chart(ctxPerformance, { type: 'line', data: { labels: ['Jan', 'Fev', 'Mar', 'Avr', 'Mai', 'Juin'], datasets: [{ label: 'Performance (%)', data: [0,0,0,0,0,0], borderColor: '#28a745', tension: 0.3 }] } });
            if (ctxHourly) charts.hourlyChart = new Chart(ctxHourly, { type: 'bar', data: { labels: [], datasets: [{ label: 'Courriers', data: [], backgroundColor: '#667eea' }] } });
            if (ctxWeekly) charts.weeklyChart = new Chart(ctxWeekly, { type: 'bar', data: { labels: ['Lun', 'Mar', 'Mer', 'Jeu', 'Ven', 'Sam', 'Dim'], datasets: [{ data: [0,0,0,0,0,0,0], backgroundColor: '#764ba2' }] } });
            if (ctxProcessing) charts.processingChart = new Chart(ctxProcessing, { type: 'bar', data: { labels: ['Haute', 'Moyenne', 'Basse'], datasets: [{ label: 'Temps (heures)', data: [0,0,0], backgroundColor: '#28a745' }] } });
            if (ctxML) charts.mlChart = new Chart(ctxML, { type: 'line', data: { labels: [], datasets: [{ label: 'Prédiction', data: [], borderColor: '#dc3545', tension: 0.4 }] } });
        }

        function updateCharts(courriers) {
            const enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            const traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent').length;
            const archives = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'archive').length;
            const high = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length;
            const medium = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'medium').length;
            const low = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'low').length;
            if (charts.statusChart) { charts.statusChart.data.datasets[0].data = [enAttente, traites, urgents, archives]; charts.statusChart.update(); }
            if (charts.priorityChart) { charts.priorityChart.data.datasets[0].data = [high, medium, low]; charts.priorityChart.update(); }
        }

        async function loadStats() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/stats/global', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    if (data.evolution && charts.monthlyChart) { charts.monthlyChart.data.labels = data.evolution.map(e => e.mois); charts.monthlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.evolution.map(e => e.total); charts.monthlyChart.update(); }
                    if (data.performance && charts.performanceChart) { charts.performanceChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.performance; charts.performanceChart.update(); }
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur stats:', error); }
        }

        async function loadAdvancedAnalytics() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/analytics/advanced', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    if (data.hourly_distribution && charts.hourlyChart) { const hours = Array(24).fill(0); data.hourly_distribution.forEach(h => { if (h.heure >= 0 && h.heure < 24) hours[h.heure] = h.total; }); charts.hourlyChart.data.labels = hours.map((_, i) => `${i}h`); charts.hourlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = hours; charts.hourlyChart.update(); }
                    if (data.weekly_distribution && charts.weeklyChart) { const joursMap = { 'Lundi':0, 'Mardi':1, 'Mercredi':2, 'Jeudi':3, 'Vendredi':4, 'Samedi':5, 'Dimanche':6 }; const valeurs = [0,0,0,0,0,0,0]; data.weekly_distribution.forEach(d => { const idx = joursMap[d.jour]; if (idx !== undefined) valeurs[idx] = d.total; }); charts.weeklyChart.data.datasets[0].data = valeurs; charts.weeklyChart.update(); }
                    if (data.processing_time && charts.processingChart) { const prioriteMap = { 'high':0, 'medium':1, 'low':2 }; const valeurs = [0,0,0]; data.processing_time.forEach(p => { const idx = prioriteMap[p.priorite]; if (idx !== undefined) valeurs[idx] = p.temps_moyen || 0; }); charts.processingChart.data.datasets[0].data = valeurs; charts.processingChart.update(); }
                }
                await loadMLPredictions();
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur analytics:', error); }
        }

        async function loadMLPredictions() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/ml/predictions', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success && charts.mlChart) {
                    charts.mlChart.data.labels = data.jours_labels;
                    charts.mlChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.predictions_30j;
                    charts.mlChart.update();
                    const suggestionsDiv = document.getElementById('mlSuggestionsList');
                    if (suggestionsDiv && data.suggestions) { suggestionsDiv.innerHTML = data.suggestions.map(s => `<div style="padding:10px;margin:5px 0;background:#f8f9fa;border-radius:8px;border-left:3px solid #667eea;">💡 ${s}</div>`).join(''); }
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur ML:', error); }
        }

        async function loadAuditLogs() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('auditLogsBody');
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/audit/logs', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success && data.logs) { tbody.innerHTML = data.logs.map(log => `<tr><td>${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}</td><td>${escapeHtml(log.username)}</span></span></td><td><span class="badge badge-en_attente">${log.action}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(log.details || '-')}</td><td>${log.ip_address || '-'}</td></tr>`).join(''); }
                else { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Non autorisé</span></span></td></span>'; }
            } catch (error) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Erreur</span></span></td></span>'; }
        }

        function refreshAuditLogs() { loadAuditLogs(); }

        // ==================== ASSIGNATION ====================
        async function loadAssignationInterface() {
            if (userRole === 'directeur') {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                await loadPendingCourriers();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                await loadMyAssignedCourriers();
            }
            await loadNotifications();
        }

        async function loadPendingCourriers() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    pendingCourriers = data.courriers;
                    servicesUsers = data.services_users;
                    displayPendingCourriers(pendingCourriers);
                }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur chargement courriers', 'error'); }
        }

        function displayPendingCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingCourriersTable');
            if (!courriers || courriers.length === 0) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center">✅ Aucun courrier en attente d\'assignation</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${c.id}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 50))}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openAssignModal(${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#667eea;">📨 Assigner</button></td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        function filterCourriersAssign() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchCourrierAssign').value.toLowerCase();
            const priority = document.getElementById('filterPrioriteAssign').value;
            let filtered = pendingCourriers;
            if (search) filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.expediteur.toLowerCase().includes(search) || c.objet.toLowerCase().includes(search));
            if (priority !== 'all') filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.priorite === priority);
            displayPendingCourriers(filtered);
        }

        function openAssignModal(courrierId) {
            const courrier = pendingCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId);
            if (!courrier) return;
            currentAssignCourrier = courrier;
            document.getElementById('assignCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div style="background:#f8f9fa; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; margin-bottom:15px;"><p><strong>📄 Courrier #${courrier.id}</strong></p><p>📌 Objet: ${escapeHtml(courrier.objet)}</p><p>📅 Date: ${courrier.date_reception}</p><p>👤 Expéditeur: ${escapeHtml(courrier.expediteur)}</p><p>⚡ Priorité: ${courrier.priorite === 'high' ? '🔴 Haute' : courrier.priorite === 'medium' ? '🟡 Moyenne' : '🟢 Basse'}</p></div>`;
            const select = document.getElementById('assignToUser');
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Sélectionner un utilisateur --</option>';
            for (const [service, users] of Object.entries(servicesUsers)) {
                const optgroup = document.createElement('optgroup');
                optgroup.label = `🏢 ${service}`;
                users.forEach(u => { const option = document.createElement('option'); option.value = u.id; option.textContent = `${u.nom_complet} (${u.role})`; optgroup.appendChild(option); });
                select.appendChild(optgroup);
            }
            document.getElementById('assignComment').value = '';
            document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmAssign() {
            const userId = document.getElementById('assignToUser').value;
            const commentaire = document.getElementById('assignComment').value;
            if (!userId) { showNotification('Veuillez sélectionner un utilisateur', 'error'); return; }
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/assignations/assign', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, credentials: 'include', body: JSON.stringify({ courrier_id: currentAssignCourrier.id, assigned_to_user_id: userId, commentaire: commentaire }) });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { showNotification(data.message, 'success'); closeAssignModal(); await loadPendingCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); }
                else { showNotification(data.message, 'error'); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        async function loadMyAssignedCourriers() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/assignations/my-courriers', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { displayMyAssignedCourriers(data.courriers); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        function displayMyAssignedCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('myAssignedCourriersTable');
            if (!courriers || courriers.length === 0) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center">📭 Aucun courrier assigné</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${c.id}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0, 50))}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.assigned_by || '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.assignation_commentaire ? escapeHtml(c.assignation_commentaire.substring(0, 50)) : '-'}</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>
                    <td>${c.assignation_status === 'pending' ? `<button class="btn-add" onclick="openTraiterModal(${c.assignation_id}, ${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#28a745;">✅ Traiter</button>` : '<span class="badge badge-success">Traité</span>'}</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        function openTraiterModal(assignationId, courrierId) {
            currentTraiterAssignation = { assignationId, courrierId };
            const courrier = allCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId);
            if (courrier) { document.getElementById('traiterCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div style="background:#f8f9fa; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; margin-bottom:15px;"><p><strong>📄 Courrier #${courrier.id}</strong></p><p>📌 Objet: ${escapeHtml(courrier.objet)}</p><p>👤 Expéditeur: ${escapeHtml(courrier.expediteur)}</p></div>`; }
            document.getElementById('traiterComment').value = '';
            document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmTraiter() {
            const commentaire = document.getElementById('traiterComment').value;
            if (!commentaire) { showNotification('Veuillez ajouter un rapport', 'error'); return; }
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`/api/assignations/${currentTraiterAssignation.assignationId}/traiter`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, credentials: 'include', body: JSON.stringify({ commentaire: commentaire }) });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { showNotification('Courrier traité !', 'success'); closeTraiterModal(); await loadMyAssignedCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats(); }
                else { showNotification(data.message, 'error'); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== NOTIFICATIONS ====================
        async function loadNotifications() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    const notifCount = document.getElementById('notifCount');
                    if (data.total > 0) { notifCount.textContent = data.total > 9 ? '9+' : data.total; notifCount.style.display = 'inline-block'; }
                    else { notifCount.style.display = 'none'; }
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error('Erreur:', error); }
        }

        function showNotifications() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('notificationsModal');
            const container = document.getElementById('notificationsList');
            fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials: 'include' })
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.success && data.notifications.length > 0) {
                        container.innerHTML = data.notifications.map(n => `<div style="padding:12px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee; background:${n.is_read ? '#fff' : '#e3f2fd'}; border-radius:8px; margin-bottom:8px;"><p><strong>📨 ${n.type === 'assignation' ? 'Nouvelle assignation' : 'Traitement terminé'}</strong></p><p style="font-size:13px; color:#666;">${n.message}</p><p style="font-size:11px; color:#999; margin-top:5px;">${n.created_at}</p></div>`).join('');
                    } else { container.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; color:#999;">Aucune notification</p>'; }
                });
            modal.classList.add('active');
        }

        // ==================== COURRIER ACTIONS ====================
        function viewCourrier(id) {
            const c = allCourriers.find(c => c.id === id);
            if (c) { alert(`📄 DÉTAIL COURRIER #${c.id}\n\nExpéditeur: ${c.expediteur}\nObjet: ${c.objet}\nDate: ${c.date_reception}\nPriorité: ${c.priorite}\nStatut: ${c.statut}`); if (c.scan_image_path && confirm('Voir le scan ?')) viewScan(c.id); }
        }

        async function editCourrier(id) {
            const newObjet = prompt('Nouvel objet:');
            if (newObjet) {
                try { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, credentials: 'include', body: JSON.stringify({ objet: newObjet }) }); showNotification('Courrier modifié', 'success'); loadCourriers(); }
                catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
            }
        }

        async function deleteCourrier(id) {
            if (!confirm('Supprimer ce courrier ?')) return;
            try { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' }); showNotification('Courrier supprimé', 'success'); loadCourriers(); }
            catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        async function viewScan(id) { window.open(`/api/scan/view/${id}`, '_blank'); }

        // ==================== SCAN ====================
        function openScanModal() {
            if (!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante', 'error'); return; }
            document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.add('active');
            resetScanModal();
        }

        function closeScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.remove('active'); resetScanModal(); }

        function resetScanModal() {
            document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('fileInput').value = '';
            currentScanFile = null;
            currentAnalysis = null;
        }

        let currentScanFile = null;
        let currentAnalysis = null;

        function handleFileUpload(file) {
            if (!file) return;
            currentScanFile = file;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) { document.getElementById('previewImage').src = e.target.result; document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'block'; };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            analyzeDocument(file);
        }

        async function analyzeDocument(file) {
            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('document', file);
            document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'none';
            let progress = 0;
            const interval = setInterval(() => { progress += 10; document.getElementById('scanProgressFill').style.width = progress + '%'; if (progress >= 100) clearInterval(interval); }, 150);
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/scan/analyze', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    currentAnalysis = data;
                    document.getElementById('extractedText').innerHTML = data.extracted_text;
                    document.getElementById('verdictBadge').innerHTML = `<strong>Score: ${data.confidence}%</strong> | ${data.processing_time}ms`;
                    document.getElementById('verdictBadge').style.background = data.confidence >= 70 ? '#d4edda' : data.confidence >= 40 ? '#fff3cd' : '#f8d7da';
                    document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'block';
                } else { showNotification(data.message, 'error'); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur analyse', 'error'); }
            finally { clearInterval(interval); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none'; }
        }

        async function saveScannedCourrier() {
            if (!currentScanFile || !currentAnalysis) { showNotification('Aucun document analysé', 'error'); return; }
            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            const courrierData = {
                date_reception: today,
                expediteur: currentAnalysis.suggested_expediteur || 'Document scanné',
                objet: currentAnalysis.suggested_objet || 'Courrier scanné',
                contenu: currentAnalysis.extracted_text,
                priorite: currentAnalysis.suggested_priorite || 'medium',
                numero_reference: currentAnalysis.numero_reference || ''
            };
            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('scan_image', currentScanFile);
            formData.append('courrier_data', JSON.stringify(courrierData));
            formData.append('analysis_score', currentAnalysis.confidence);
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/scan/save', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { showNotification('Courrier enregistré !', 'success'); closeScanModal(); loadCourriers(); loadStats(); }
                else { showNotification(data.message, 'error'); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== EXPORT & ARCHIVE ====================
        async function exportAllToPDF() {
            document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '<span style="color:#17a2b8;">Génération PDF...</span>';
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/export/pdf', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, credentials: 'include', body: JSON.stringify({ courrier_ids: [] }) });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = `<a href="${data.pdf_url}" target="_blank">📄 Télécharger PDF</a>`; showNotification('PDF généré', 'success'); }
                else { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; }
            } catch (error) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; }
        }

        async function archiveAuto() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/archive/auto', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) { showNotification(data.message, 'success'); loadCourriers(); }
                else { showNotification(data.message, 'error'); }
            } catch (error) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== PAGINATION ====================
        function previousPage() { if (currentPage > 1) { currentPage--; loadCourriers(); } }
        function nextPage() { if (currentPage < totalPages) { currentPage++; loadCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== TABS ====================
        function initTabs() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const tabId = item.dataset.tab;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
                    item.classList.add('active');
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.remove('active'));
                    document.getElementById(`${tabId}Tab`).classList.add('active');
                    if (tabId === 'courriers') loadCourriers();
                    if (tabId === 'assignation') loadAssignationInterface();
                    if (tabId === 'traitement') { loadTraitementCourriers(); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
                    if (tabId === 'statistiques') loadStats();
                    if (tabId === 'analytics') { loadAdvancedAnalytics(); loadMLPredictions(); }
                    if (tabId === 'audit') loadAuditLogs();
                });
            });
        }

        // ==================== UTILITAIRES ====================
        function getPriorityBadge(p) {
            if (p === 'high') return '<span class="priority-high">🔴 Haute</span>';
            if (p === 'medium') return '<span class="priority-medium">🟡 Moyenne</span>';
            return '<span class="priority-low">🟢 Basse</span>';
        }

        function getStatusBadge(s) {
            const badges = { en_attente: 'badge-en_attente', traite: 'badge-traite', urgent: 'badge-urgent', archive: 'badge-archive' };
            const icons = { en_attente: '⏳', traite: '✅', urgent: '🚨', archive: '📦' };
            return `<span class="badge ${badges[s] || 'badge-en_attente'}">${icons[s] || '📋'} ${s?.replace('_', ' ') || 'En attente'}</span>`;
        }

        function escapeHtml(t) { if (!t) return ''; const div = document.createElement('div'); div.textContent = t; return div.innerHTML; }
        function showNotification(msg, type) { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = `notification ${type}`; n.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(n); setTimeout(() => n.remove(), 3000); }
        async function logout() { await fetch('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }); window.location.href = '/'; }
        function closeAssignModal() { document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeNotificationsModal() { document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeMultipleTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }

        // Drag & drop
        const uploadArea = document.getElementById('uploadArea');
        if (uploadArea) {
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.style.background = '#e3f2fd'; });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragleave', () => { uploadArea.style.background = '#f8f9fa'; });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('drop', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.style.background = '#f8f9fa'; const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]; if (file) handleFileUpload(file); });
        }

        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
