    <div id="notificationsModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>🔔 Notifications</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeNotificationsModal()">&times;</span></div><div id="notificationsList"></div></div></div>

    <script>
        // ==================== VARIABLES ====================
        let currentPage = 1, totalPages = 1;
        let currentPageTraitement = 1, totalPagesTraitement = 1;
        let traitementCourriers = [], selectedTraitementIds = [], currentTraitementFilter = 'all';
        let charts = {}, userPermissions = {}, userRole = '', userService = '';
        let allCourriers = [], pendingCourriers = [], servicesUsers = {};
        let currentAssignCourrier = null, currentTraiterAssignation = null;
        let currentScanFile = null, currentAnalysis = null;

        // ==================== VÉRIFICATION AUTH ====================
        async function checkAuth() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/check_auth', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (!data.success) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
                return data;
            } catch (error) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
        }

        // ==================== CHARGEMENT DES DONNÉES ====================
        async function loadAllData() {
            const auth = await checkAuth();
            if (!auth) return;
            
            if (auth.user) {
                document.getElementById('username').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userName').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userRole').textContent = auth.user.role || 'Utilisateur';
                document.getElementById('userService').textContent = auth.user.service || '';
                document.getElementById('userAvatar').textContent = (auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username).charAt(0).toUpperCase();
                userRole = auth.user.role;
                userService = auth.user.service;
            }
            
            await loadUserPermissions();
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
            await loadAdvancedAnalytics();
            await loadAuditLogs();
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            initCharts();
            setupTabs();
            loadAssignationInterface();
            loadNotifications();
            setInterval(() => { loadNotifications(); }, 30000);
        }

        async function loadUserPermissions() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/user/permissions', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    userPermissions = data.permissions;
                    if (!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) document.getElementById('btnAddCourrier').style.display = 'none';
                    if (data.role !== 'directeur') document.getElementById('menuAudit').style.display = 'none';
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); }
        }

        // ==================== COURRIERS ====================
        async function loadCourriers() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '';
            const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const priority = document.getElementById('priorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
            let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPage}&per_page=10`;
            if (search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
            if (status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
            if (priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
            try {
                const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayCourriersTable(allCourriers);
                    displayRecentCourriers(allCourriers);
                    updateStatsCards(allCourriers);
                    updateCharts(allCourriers);
                    totalPages = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfo').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = currentPage <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtn').disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
                    document.getElementById('courrierBadge').textContent = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); }
        }

        function displayCourriersTable(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('courriersTableBody');
            if (!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td><button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button>${userPermissions.can_edit_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="editCourrier(${c.id})">✏️</button>` : ''}${userPermissions.can_delete_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-delete" onclick="deleteCourrier(${c.id})">🗑️</button>` : ''}${c.scan_image_path ? `<button class="action-btn btn-scan" onclick="viewScan(${c.id})">📄</button>` : ''}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function displayRecentCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recentCourriers');
            if (!tbody) return;
            const recent = [...courriers].slice(-5).reverse();
            tbody.innerHTML = recent.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateStatsCards(courriers) {
            document.getElementById('statTotal').innerHTML = courriers.length;
            document.getElementById('statEnAttente').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            document.getElementById('statTraites').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            document.getElementById('statUrgents').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent' || c.priorite === 'high').length;
            document.getElementById('traitementBadge').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
        }

        function initCharts() {
            const ctxStatus = document.getElementById('statusChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPriority = document.getElementById('priorityChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxMonthly = document.getElementById('monthlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxTop = document.getElementById('topExpediteursChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxHourly = document.getElementById('hourlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxML = document.getElementById('mlPredictionChart')?.getContext('2d');
            if(ctxStatus) charts.statusChart = new Chart(ctxStatus, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['En attente','Traité','Urgent','Archivé'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ffc107','#28a745','#dc3545','#17a2b8'] }] } });
            if(ctxPriority) charts.priorityChart = new Chart(ctxPriority, { type:'pie', data:{ labels:['Haute','Moyenne','Basse'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#dc3545','#ffc107','#28a745'] }] } });
            if(ctxMonthly) charts.monthlyChart = new Chart(ctxMonthly, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:[], borderColor:'#1e3c72' }] } });
            if(ctxTop) charts.topExpediteursChart = new Chart(ctxTop, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ data:[], backgroundColor:'#2a5298' }] } });
            if(ctxHourly) charts.hourlyChart = new Chart(ctxHourly, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>`${i}h`), datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:Array(24).fill(0), backgroundColor:'#1e3c72' }] } });
            if(ctxML) charts.mlChart = new Chart(ctxML, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Prédiction', data:[], borderColor:'#dc3545' }] } });
        }

        function updateCharts(courriers) {
            const enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            const traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent').length;
            const archives = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'archive').length;
            const high = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length;
            const medium = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'medium').length;
            const low = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'low').length;
            if(charts.statusChart) { charts.statusChart.data.datasets[0].data = [enAttente, traites, urgents, archives]; charts.statusChart.update(); }
            if(charts.priorityChart) { charts.priorityChart.data.datasets[0].data = [high, medium, low]; charts.priorityChart.update(); }
        }

        async function loadStats() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/stats/global', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json(); 
            if(data.success && data.evolution && charts.monthlyChart) { charts.monthlyChart.data.labels = data.evolution.map(e => e.mois); charts.monthlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.evolution.map(e => e.total); charts.monthlyChart.update(); } 
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAdvancedAnalytics() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/analytics/advanced', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.hourly_distribution && charts.hourlyChart) { const hours = Array(24).fill(0); data.hourly_distribution.forEach(h => { if(h.heure >=0 && h.heure<24) hours[h.heure] = h.total; }); charts.hourlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = hours; charts.hourlyChart.update(); }
            await loadMLPredictions(); } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadMLPredictions() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/ml/predictions', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && charts.mlChart) { charts.mlChart.data.labels = data.jours_labels; charts.mlChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.predictions_30j; charts.mlChart.update(); 
            const suggestionsDiv = document.getElementById('mlSuggestionsList'); if(suggestionsDiv && data.suggestions) { suggestionsDiv.innerHTML = data.suggestions.map(s => `<div style="padding:10px;margin:5px 0;background:#f8f9fa;border-radius:10px;border-left:3px solid #1e3c72;">💡 ${s}</div>`).join(''); } } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAuditLogs() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('auditLogsBody');
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/audit/logs', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.logs) { tbody.innerHTML = data.logs.slice(0,20).map(log => `<tr><td>${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}</td><td>${escapeHtml(log.username)}</td><td><span class="badge badge-en_attente">${log.action}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(log.details || '-')}</td><td>${log.ip_address || '-'}</td></tr>`).join(''); }
            else { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Non autorisé</td></tr>'; } } catch(e) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Erreur</td></tr>'; } }
        function refreshAuditLogs() { loadAuditLogs(); }

        // ==================== TRAITEMENT ====================
        async function loadTraitementCourriers() {
            try { let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPageTraitement}&per_page=10`;
            if(currentTraitementFilter !== 'all') url += `&status=${currentTraitementFilter}`;
            const res = await fetch(url, { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { traitementCourriers = data.courriers || []; displayTraitementCourriers(traitementCourriers); totalPagesTraitement = data.pages || 1;
            document.getElementById('pageInfoTraitement').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPageTraitement} / ${totalPagesTraitement}`;
            document.getElementById('prevBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement <= 1;
            document.getElementById('nextBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement >= totalPagesTraitement; } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        function displayTraitementCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('traitementCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="traitement-checkbox" value="${c.id}" ${c.statut === 'traite' ? 'disabled' : ''} onchange="updateTraitementSelectedCount()"></td><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateTraitementSelectedCount() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:checked:not([disabled])');
            selectedTraitementIds = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ ${selectedTraitementIds.length} sélectionné(s)`;
            const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            if(selectAll) selectAll.checked = checkboxes.length === allCheckboxes.length;
        }

        function toggleSelectAllTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = !cb.checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function toggleSelectAllFromCheckboxTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox').checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function filterTraitementCourriers(f) { currentTraitementFilter = f; currentPageTraitement = 1; document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active')); event.target.classList.add('active'); loadTraitementCourriers(); }
        function openMultipleTraiterModal() { if(!selectedTraitementIds.length) { showNotification('Sélectionnez des courriers','error'); return; } document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmMultipleTraiter() {
            if(!document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; }
            let s=0,e=0;
            for(const id of selectedTraitementIds) {
                try{ const res=await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`,{method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({statut:'traite'})}); if((await res.json()).success) s++; else e++; } catch(err){ e++; }
            }
            showNotification(`✅ ${s} traités, ❌ ${e} erreurs`, s>0?'success':'error');
            closeMultipleTraiterModal(); selectedTraitementIds = [];
            await loadTraitementCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats();
        }
        function previousPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement > 1) { currentPageTraitement--; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }
        function nextPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement < totalPagesTraitement) { currentPageTraitement++; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== ASSIGNATION ====================
        async function loadAssignationInterface() {
            if(userRole === 'directeur') {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                await loadPendingCourriers();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                await loadMyAssignedCourriers();
            }
        }

        async function loadPendingCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { pendingCourriers = data.courriers; servicesUsers = data.services_users; displayPendingCourriers(pendingCourriers); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur chargement', 'error'); } }

        function displayPendingCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openAssignModal(${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#667eea;">📨 Assigner</button></td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function filterCourriersAssign() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchCourrierAssign').value.toLowerCase();
            const priority = document.getElementById('filterPrioriteAssign').value;
            let filtered = pendingCourriers;
            if(search) filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.expediteur.toLowerCase().includes(search) || c.objet.toLowerCase().includes(search));
            if(priority !== 'all') filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.priorite === priority);
            displayPendingCourriers(filtered);
        }

        function openAssignModal(courrierId) {
            const c = pendingCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId);
            if(!c) return;
            currentAssignCourrier = c;
            document.getElementById('assignCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div style="background:#f8f9fa; padding:10px; border-radius:10px;"><strong>Courrier #${c.id}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(c.objet)}<br>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}<br>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</div>`;
            const select = document.getElementById('assignToUser');
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choisir --</option>';
            for(const [service, users] of Object.entries(servicesUsers)) {
                const group = document.createElement('optgroup');
                group.label = service;
                users.forEach(u => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = u.id; opt.textContent = `${u.nom_complet} (${u.role})`; group.appendChild(opt); });
                select.appendChild(group);
            }
            document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmAssign() {
            const userId = document.getElementById('assignToUser').value;
            if(!userId) { showNotification('Choisissez un utilisateur', 'error'); return; }
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/assign', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ courrier_id:currentAssignCourrier.id, assigned_to_user_id:userId, commentaire:document.getElementById('assignComment').value }) });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) { showNotification(data.message,'success'); closeAssignModal(); await loadPendingCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); }
                else showNotification(data.message,'error');
            } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        async function loadMyAssignedCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/my-courriers', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { const tbody = document.getElementById('myAssignedCourriersTable');
            if(!data.courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = data.courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${c.assigned_by || '-'}</td><td>${c.assignation_commentaire ? escapeHtml(c.assignation_commentaire.substring(0,30)) : '-'}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openTraiterModal(${c.assignation_id}, ${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#28a745;">✅ Traiter</button></td></tr>`).join(''); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); } }

        function openTraiterModal(assignId, courId) { currentTraiterAssignation = { assignationId:assignId, courrierId:courId }; document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmTraiter() {
            if(!document.getElementById('traiterComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; }
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/assignations/${currentTraiterAssignation.assignationId}/traiter`, { method:'PUT', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ commentaire:document.getElementById('traiterComment').value }) });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) { showNotification('Courrier traité !','success'); closeTraiterModal(); await loadMyAssignedCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats(); }
                else showNotification(data.message,'error');
            } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== NOTIFICATIONS ====================
        async function loadNotifications() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { const notifCount = document.getElementById('notifCount');
            if(data.total>0) { notifCount.textContent = data.total>9?'9+':data.total; notifCount.style.display='inline-block'; } else notifCount.style.display='none'; } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }
        function showNotifications() { fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }).then(r=>r.json()).then(data=>{ document.getElementById('notificationsList').innerHTML = data.success && data.notifications.length ? data.notifications.map(n=>`<div style="padding:12px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee; background:${n.is_read?'#fff':'#e3f2fd'}; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>📨 ${n.type==='assignation'?'Nouvelle assignation':'Traitement terminé'}</strong><p>${n.message}</p><small>${n.created_at}</small></div>`).join('') : '<p>Aucune notification</p>'; document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.add('active'); }); }

        // ==================== COURRIER ACTIONS ====================
        function viewCourrier(id) { const c = allCourriers.find(c => c.id === id); if(c) alert(`Courrier #${c.id}\nExpéditeur: ${c.expediteur}\nObjet: ${c.objet}\nDate: ${c.date_reception}\nPriorité: ${c.priorite}\nStatut: ${c.statut}`); }
        async function editCourrier(id) { const newObjet = prompt('Nouvel objet:'); if(newObjet) { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method:'PUT', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ objet:newObjet }) }); showNotification('Modifié','success'); loadCourriers(); } }
        async function deleteCourrier(id) { if(confirm('Supprimer ?')) { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method:'DELETE', credentials:'include' }); showNotification('Supprimé','success'); loadCourriers(); } }
        async function viewScan(id) { window.open(`/api/scan/view/${id}`,'_blank'); }
        function previousPage() { if(currentPage > 1) { currentPage--; loadCourriers(); } }
        function nextPage() { if(currentPage < totalPages) { currentPage++; loadCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== SCAN ====================
        function openScanModal() { if(!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante','error'); return; } document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.add('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function closeScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.remove('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function resetScanModal() { document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('fileInput').value = ''; currentScanFile = null; currentAnalysis = null; }
        function handleFileUpload(file) { if(!file) return; currentScanFile = file; const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = e => { document.getElementById('previewImage').src = e.target.result; document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'block'; }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); analyzeDocument(file); }
        async function analyzeDocument(file) { const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('document', file); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'block'; let progress = 0; const interval = setInterval(() => { progress += 10; document.getElementById('scanProgressFill').style.width = progress+'%'; if(progress>=100) clearInterval(interval); }, 150); try { const res = await fetch('/api/scan/analyze', { method:'POST', credentials:'include', body:fd }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { currentAnalysis = data; document.getElementById('extractedText').innerHTML = data.extracted_text; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'block'; } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur analyse','error'); } finally { clearInterval(interval); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none'; } }
        async function saveScannedCourrier() { if(!currentScanFile||!currentAnalysis) { showNotification('Aucun document analysé','error'); return; } const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('scan_image', currentScanFile); fd.append('courrier_data', JSON.stringify({ date_reception:new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], expediteur:currentAnalysis.suggested_expediteur||'Document scanné', objet:currentAnalysis.suggested_objet||'Courrier scanné', contenu:currentAnalysis.extracted_text, priorite:currentAnalysis.suggested_priorite||'medium' })); const res = await fetch('/api/scan/save', { method:'POST', credentials:'include', body:fd }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { showNotification('Courrier enregistré !','success'); closeScanModal(); loadCourriers(); loadStats(); } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); }

        // ==================== EXPORT & ARCHIVE ====================
        async function exportAllToPDF() { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = 'Génération...'; try { const res = await fetch('/api/export/pdf', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ courrier_ids:[] }) }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = `<a href="${data.pdf_url}" target="_blank">📄 Télécharger PDF</a>`; showNotification('PDF généré','success'); } else document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; } catch(e) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; } }
        async function archiveAuto() { try { const res = await fetch('/api/archive/auto', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { showNotification(data.message,'success'); loadCourriers(); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur','error'); } }

        // ==================== UTILITAIRES ====================
        function getPriorityBadge(p) { if(p==='high') return '<span class="priority-high">🔴 Haute</span>'; if(p==='medium') return '<span class="priority-medium">🟡 Moyenne</span>'; return '<span class="priority-low">🟢 Basse</span>'; }
        function getStatusBadge(s) { const b = { en_attente:'badge-en_attente', traite:'badge-traite', urgent:'badge-urgent', archive:'badge-archive' }; const i = { en_attente:'⏳', traite:'✅', urgent:'🚨', archive:'📦' }; return `<span class="badge ${b[s]||'badge-en_attente'}">${i[s]||'📋'} ${s?.replace('_',' ')||'En attente'}</span>`; }
        function escapeHtml(t) { if(!t) return ''; const div = document.createElement('div'); div.textContent = t; return div.innerHTML; }
        function showNotification(msg, type) { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = `notification ${type}`; n.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(n); setTimeout(()=>n.remove(),3000); }
        async function logout() { await fetch('/api/logout', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); window.location.href = '/'; }
        function closeAssignModal() { document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeNotificationsModal() { document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeMultipleTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }

        function setupTabs() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const tabId = item.dataset.tab;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
                    item.classList.add('active');
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.remove('active'));
                    document.getElementById(`${tabId}Tab`).classList.add('active');
                    if(tabId === 'courriers') loadCourriers();
                    if(tabId === 'assignation') loadAssignationInterface();
                    if(tabId === 'traitement') loadTraitementCourriers();
                    if(tabId === 'statistiques') loadStats();
                    if(tabId === 'analytics') { loadAdvancedAnalytics(); loadMLPredictions(); }
                    if(tabId === 'audit') loadAuditLogs();
                });
            });
        }

        // Démarrer l'application
        loadAllData();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        // ==================== VARIABLES ====================
        let currentPage = 1, totalPages = 1;
        let currentPageTraitement = 1, totalPagesTraitement = 1;
        let traitementCourriers = [], selectedTraitementIds = [], currentTraitementFilter = 'all';
        let charts = {}, userPermissions = {}, userRole = '', userService = '';
        let allCourriers = [], pendingCourriers = [], servicesUsers = {};
        let currentAssignCourrier = null, currentTraiterAssignation = null;
        let currentScanFile = null, currentAnalysis = null;

        // ==================== VÉRIFICATION AUTH ====================
        async function checkAuth() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/check_auth', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (!data.success) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
                return data;
            } catch (error) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
        }

        // ==================== CHARGEMENT DES DONNÉES ====================
        async function loadAllData() {
            const auth = await checkAuth();
            if (!auth) return;
            
            if (auth.user) {
                document.getElementById('username').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userName').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userRole').textContent = auth.user.role || 'Utilisateur';
                document.getElementById('userService').textContent = auth.user.service || '';
                document.getElementById('userAvatar').textContent = (auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username).charAt(0).toUpperCase();
                userRole = auth.user.role;
                userService = auth.user.service;
            }
            
            await loadUserPermissions();
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
            await loadAdvancedAnalytics();
            await loadAuditLogs();
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            initCharts();
            setupTabs();
            loadAssignationInterface();
            loadNotifications();
            setInterval(() => { loadNotifications(); }, 30000);
        }

        async function loadUserPermissions() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/user/permissions', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    userPermissions = data.permissions;
                    if (!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) document.getElementById('btnAddCourrier').style.display = 'none';
                    if (data.role !== 'directeur') document.getElementById('menuAudit').style.display = 'none';
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); }
        }

        // ==================== COURRIERS ====================
        async function loadCourriers() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '';
            const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const priority = document.getElementById('priorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
            let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPage}&per_page=10`;
            if (search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
            if (status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
            if (priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
            try {
                const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayCourriersTable(allCourriers);
                    displayRecentCourriers(allCourriers);
                    updateStatsCards(allCourriers);
                    updateCharts(allCourriers);
                    totalPages = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfo').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = currentPage <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtn').disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
                    document.getElementById('courrierBadge').textContent = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
                }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); }
        }

        function displayCourriersTable(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('courriersTableBody');
            if (!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td><button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button>${userPermissions.can_edit_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="editCourrier(${c.id})">✏️</button>` : ''}${userPermissions.can_delete_courrier ? `<button class="action-btn btn-delete" onclick="deleteCourrier(${c.id})">🗑️</button>` : ''}${c.scan_image_path ? `<button class="action-btn btn-scan" onclick="viewScan(${c.id})">📄</button>` : ''}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function displayRecentCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recentCourriers');
            if (!tbody) return;
            const recent = [...courriers].slice(-5).reverse();
            tbody.innerHTML = recent.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateStatsCards(courriers) {
            document.getElementById('statTotal').innerHTML = courriers.length;
            document.getElementById('statEnAttente').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            document.getElementById('statTraites').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            document.getElementById('statUrgents').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent' || c.priorite === 'high').length;
            document.getElementById('traitementBadge').innerHTML = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
        }

        function initCharts() {
            const ctxStatus = document.getElementById('statusChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPriority = document.getElementById('priorityChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxMonthly = document.getElementById('monthlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxTop = document.getElementById('topExpediteursChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxHourly = document.getElementById('hourlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxML = document.getElementById('mlPredictionChart')?.getContext('2d');
            if(ctxStatus) charts.statusChart = new Chart(ctxStatus, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['En attente','Traité','Urgent','Archivé'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#f59e0b','#10b981','#ef4444','#06b6d4'] }] } });
            if(ctxPriority) charts.priorityChart = new Chart(ctxPriority, { type:'pie', data:{ labels:['Haute','Moyenne','Basse'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ef4444','#f59e0b','#10b981'] }] } });
            if(ctxMonthly) charts.monthlyChart = new Chart(ctxMonthly, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:[], borderColor:'#7c3aed', tension:0.3 }] } });
            if(ctxTop) charts.topExpediteursChart = new Chart(ctxTop, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ data:[], backgroundColor:'#a855f7' }] } });
            if(ctxHourly) charts.hourlyChart = new Chart(ctxHourly, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>`${i}h`), datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:Array(24).fill(0), backgroundColor:'#7c3aed' }] } });
            if(ctxML) charts.mlChart = new Chart(ctxML, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Prédiction', data:[], borderColor:'#dc3545', tension:0.4 }] } });
        }

        function updateCharts(courriers) {
            const enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            const traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent').length;
            const archives = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'archive').length;
            const high = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length;
            const medium = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'medium').length;
            const low = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'low').length;
            if(charts.statusChart) { charts.statusChart.data.datasets[0].data = [enAttente, traites, urgents, archives]; charts.statusChart.update(); }
            if(charts.priorityChart) { charts.priorityChart.data.datasets[0].data = [high, medium, low]; charts.priorityChart.update(); }
        }

        async function loadStats() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/stats/global', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json(); 
            if(data.success && data.evolution && charts.monthlyChart) { charts.monthlyChart.data.labels = data.evolution.map(e => e.mois); charts.monthlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.evolution.map(e => e.total); charts.monthlyChart.update(); } 
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAdvancedAnalytics() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/analytics/advanced', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.hourly_distribution && charts.hourlyChart) { const hours = Array(24).fill(0); data.hourly_distribution.forEach(h => { if(h.heure >=0 && h.heure<24) hours[h.heure] = h.total; }); charts.hourlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = hours; charts.hourlyChart.update(); }
            await loadMLPredictions(); } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadMLPredictions() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/ml/predictions', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && charts.mlChart) { charts.mlChart.data.labels = data.jours_labels; charts.mlChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.predictions_30j; charts.mlChart.update(); 
            const suggestionsDiv = document.getElementById('mlSuggestionsList'); if(suggestionsDiv && data.suggestions) { suggestionsDiv.innerHTML = data.suggestions.map(s => `<div style="padding:10px;margin:5px 0;background:#faf5ff;border-radius:10px;border-left:3px solid #7c3aed;">💡 ${s}</div>`).join(''); } } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAuditLogs() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('auditLogsBody');
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/audit/logs', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.logs) { tbody.innerHTML = data.logs.slice(0,20).map(log => `<tr><td>${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}</td><td>${escapeHtml(log.username)}</td><td><span class="badge badge-en_attente">${log.action}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(log.details || '-')}</td><td>${log.ip_address || '-'}</td></tr>`).join(''); }
            else { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Non autorisé</td></tr>'; } } catch(e) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5">Erreur</td></tr>'; } }
        function refreshAuditLogs() { loadAuditLogs(); }

        // ==================== TRAITEMENT ====================
        async function loadTraitementCourriers() {
            try { let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPageTraitement}&per_page=10`;
            if(currentTraitementFilter !== 'all') url += `&status=${currentTraitementFilter}`;
            const res = await fetch(url, { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { traitementCourriers = data.courriers || []; displayTraitementCourriers(traitementCourriers); totalPagesTraitement = data.pages || 1;
            document.getElementById('pageInfoTraitement').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPageTraitement} / ${totalPagesTraitement}`;
            document.getElementById('prevBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement <= 1;
            document.getElementById('nextBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement >= totalPagesTraitement; } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        function displayTraitementCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('traitementCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="traitement-checkbox" value="${c.id}" ${c.statut === 'traite' ? 'disabled' : ''} onchange="updateTraitementSelectedCount()"></td><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateTraitementSelectedCount() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:checked:not([disabled])');
            selectedTraitementIds = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ ${selectedTraitementIds.length} sélectionné(s)`;
            const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            if(selectAll) selectAll.checked = checkboxes.length === allCheckboxes.length;
        }

        function toggleSelectAllTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = !cb.checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function toggleSelectAllFromCheckboxTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox').checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function filterTraitementCourriers(f) { currentTraitementFilter = f; currentPageTraitement = 1; document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active')); event.target.classList.add('active'); loadTraitementCourriers(); }
        function openMultipleTraiterModal() { if(!selectedTraitementIds.length) { showNotification('Sélectionnez des courriers','error'); return; } document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmMultipleTraiter() {
            if(!document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; }
            let s=0,e=0;
            for(const id of selectedTraitementIds) {
                try{ const res=await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`,{method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({statut:'traite'})}); if((await res.json()).success) s++; else e++; } catch(err){ e++; }
            }
            showNotification(`✅ ${s} traités, ❌ ${e} erreurs`, s>0?'success':'error');
            closeMultipleTraiterModal(); selectedTraitementIds = [];
            await loadTraitementCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats();
        }
        function previousPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement > 1) { currentPageTraitement--; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }
        function nextPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement < totalPagesTraitement) { currentPageTraitement++; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== ASSIGNATION ====================
        async function loadAssignationInterface() {
            if(userRole === 'directeur') {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                await loadPendingCourriers();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'block';
                await loadMyAssignedCourriers();
            }
        }

        async function loadPendingCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { pendingCourriers = data.courriers; servicesUsers = data.services_users; displayPendingCourriers(pendingCourriers); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur chargement', 'error'); } }

        function displayPendingCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openAssignModal(${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#7c3aed;">📨 Assigner</button></td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function filterCourriersAssign() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchCourrierAssign').value.toLowerCase();
            const priority = document.getElementById('filterPrioriteAssign').value;
            let filtered = pendingCourriers;
            if(search) filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.expediteur.toLowerCase().includes(search) || c.objet.toLowerCase().includes(search));
            if(priority !== 'all') filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.priorite === priority);
            displayPendingCourriers(filtered);
        }

        function openAssignModal(courrierId) {
            const c = pendingCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId);
            if(!c) return;
            currentAssignCourrier = c;
            document.getElementById('assignCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div style="background:#faf5ff; padding:10px; border-radius:10px;"><strong>Courrier #${c.id}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(c.objet)}<br>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}<br>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</div>`;
            const select = document.getElementById('assignToUser');
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choisir --</option>';
            for(const [service, users] of Object.entries(servicesUsers)) {
                const group = document.createElement('optgroup');
                group.label = service;
                users.forEach(u => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = u.id; opt.textContent = `${u.nom_complet} (${u.role})`; group.appendChild(opt); });
                select.appendChild(group);
            }
            document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmAssign() {
            const userId = document.getElementById('assignToUser').value;
            if(!userId) { showNotification('Choisissez un utilisateur', 'error'); return; }
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/assign', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ courrier_id:currentAssignCourrier.id, assigned_to_user_id:userId, commentaire:document.getElementById('assignComment').value }) });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) { showNotification(data.message,'success'); closeAssignModal(); await loadPendingCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); }
                else showNotification(data.message,'error');
            } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        async function loadMyAssignedCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/my-courriers', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { const tbody = document.getElementById('myAssignedCourriersTable');
            if(!data.courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8">Aucun courrier</td></tr>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = data.courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${c.assigned_by || '-'}</td><td>${c.assignation_commentaire ? escapeHtml(c.assignation_commentaire.substring(0,30)) : '-'}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openTraiterModal(${c.assignation_id}, ${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#28a745;">✅ Traiter</button></td></tr>`).join(''); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); } }

        function openTraiterModal(assignId, courId) { currentTraiterAssignation = { assignationId:assignId, courrierId:courId }; document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmTraiter() {
            if(!document.getElementById('traiterComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; }
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/assignations/${currentTraiterAssignation.assignationId}/traiter`, { method:'PUT', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ commentaire:document.getElementById('traiterComment').value }) });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) { showNotification('Courrier traité !','success'); closeTraiterModal(); await loadMyAssignedCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats(); }
                else showNotification(data.message,'error');
            } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== NOTIFICATIONS ====================
        async function loadNotifications() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { const notifCount = document.getElementById('notifCount');
            if(data.total>0) { notifCount.textContent = data.total>9?'9+':data.total; notifCount.style.display='inline-block'; } else notifCount.style.display='none'; } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }
        function showNotifications() { fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }).then(r=>r.json()).then(data=>{ document.getElementById('notificationsList').innerHTML = data.success && data.notifications.length ? data.notifications.map(n=>`<div style="padding:12px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee; background:${n.is_read?'#fff':'#f3e8ff'}; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>📨 ${n.type==='assignation'?'Nouvelle assignation':'Traitement terminé'}</strong><p>${n.message}</p><small>${n.created_at}</small></div>`).join('') : '<p>Aucune notification</p>'; document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.add('active'); }); }

        // ==================== COURRIER ACTIONS ====================
        function viewCourrier(id) { const c = allCourriers.find(c => c.id === id); if(c) alert(`Courrier #${c.id}\nExpéditeur: ${c.expediteur}\nObjet: ${c.objet}\nDate: ${c.date_reception}\nPriorité: ${c.priorite}\nStatut: ${c.statut}`); }
        async function editCourrier(id) { const newObjet = prompt('Nouvel objet:'); if(newObjet) { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method:'PUT', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ objet:newObjet }) }); showNotification('Modifié','success'); loadCourriers(); } }
        async function deleteCourrier(id) { if(confirm('Supprimer ?')) { await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`, { method:'DELETE', credentials:'include' }); showNotification('Supprimé','success'); loadCourriers(); } }
        async function viewScan(id) { window.open(`/api/scan/view/${id}`,'_blank'); }
        function previousPage() { if(currentPage > 1) { currentPage--; loadCourriers(); } }
        function nextPage() { if(currentPage < totalPages) { currentPage++; loadCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== SCAN ====================
        function openScanModal() { if(!userPermissions.can_add_courrier) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante','error'); return; } document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.add('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function closeScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.remove('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function resetScanModal() { document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('fileInput').value = ''; currentScanFile = null; currentAnalysis = null; }
        function handleFileUpload(file) { if(!file) return; currentScanFile = file; const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = e => { document.getElementById('previewImage').src = e.target.result; document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display = 'block'; }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); analyzeDocument(file); }
        async function analyzeDocument(file) { const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('document', file); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'block'; let progress = 0; const interval = setInterval(() => { progress += 10; document.getElementById('scanProgressFill').style.width = progress+'%'; if(progress>=100) clearInterval(interval); }, 150); try { const res = await fetch('/api/scan/analyze', { method:'POST', credentials:'include', body:fd }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { currentAnalysis = data; document.getElementById('extractedText').innerHTML = data.extracted_text; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display = 'block'; } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur analyse','error'); } finally { clearInterval(interval); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display = 'none'; } }
        async function saveScannedCourrier() { if(!currentScanFile||!currentAnalysis) { showNotification('Aucun document analysé','error'); return; } const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('scan_image', currentScanFile); fd.append('courrier_data', JSON.stringify({ date_reception:new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], expediteur:currentAnalysis.suggested_expediteur||'Document scanné', objet:currentAnalysis.suggested_objet||'Courrier scanné', contenu:currentAnalysis.extracted_text, priorite:currentAnalysis.suggested_priorite||'medium' })); const res = await fetch('/api/scan/save', { method:'POST', credentials:'include', body:fd }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { showNotification('Courrier enregistré !','success'); closeScanModal(); loadCourriers(); loadStats(); } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); }

        // ==================== EXPORT & ARCHIVE ====================
        async function exportAllToPDF() { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = 'Génération...'; try { const res = await fetch('/api/export/pdf', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, credentials:'include', body:JSON.stringify({ courrier_ids:[] }) }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = `<a href="${data.pdf_url}" target="_blank">📄 Télécharger PDF</a>`; showNotification('PDF généré','success'); } else document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; } catch(e) { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML = '❌ Erreur'; } }
        async function archiveAuto() { try { const res = await fetch('/api/archive/auto', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { showNotification(data.message,'success'); loadCourriers(); } } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur','error'); } }

        // ==================== UTILITAIRES ====================
        function getPriorityBadge(p) { if(p==='high') return '<span class="priority-high">🔴 Haute</span>'; if(p==='medium') return '<span class="priority-medium">🟡 Moyenne</span>'; return '<span class="priority-low">🟢 Basse</span>'; }
        function getStatusBadge(s) { const b = { en_attente:'badge-en_attente', traite:'badge-traite', urgent:'badge-urgent', archive:'badge-archive' }; const i = { en_attente:'⏳', traite:'✅', urgent:'🚨', archive:'📦' }; return `<span class="badge ${b[s]||'badge-en_attente'}">${i[s]||'📋'} ${s?.replace('_',' ')||'En attente'}</span>`; }
        function escapeHtml(t) { if(!t) return ''; const div = document.createElement('div'); div.textContent = t; return div.innerHTML; }
        function showNotification(msg, type) { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = `notification ${type}`; n.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(n); setTimeout(()=>n.remove(),3000); }
        async function logout() { await fetch('/api/logout', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); window.location.href = '/'; }
        function closeAssignModal() { document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeNotificationsModal() { document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeMultipleTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }

        function setupTabs() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const tabId = item.dataset.tab;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
                    item.classList.add('active');
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.remove('active'));
                    document.getElementById(`${tabId}Tab`).classList.add('active');
                    if(tabId === 'courriers') loadCourriers();
                    if(tabId === 'assignation') loadAssignationInterface();
                    if(tabId === 'traitement') loadTraitementCourriers();
                    if(tabId === 'statistiques') loadStats();
                    if(tabId === 'analytics') { loadAdvancedAnalytics(); loadMLPredictions(); }
                    if(tabId === 'audit') loadAuditLogs();
                });
            });
        }

        // Démarrer l'application
        loadAllData();
    </script>
</body>
</html>




from flask import Flask, request, jsonify, session, render_template, send_file, redirect, url_for

from functools import wraps

# Initialisation de l'application
app = Flask(__name__)
app.config.from_object(Config)



# Configuration MySQL avec SQLAlchemy
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = Config.get_database_uri()
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE'] = Config.SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY'] = Config.SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY
app.config['MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH'] = Config.MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH


# Initialisation des extensions

CORS(app, supports_credentials=True)

os.makedirs(Config.SCAN_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(Config.ARCHIVE_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)


# ==================== DÉCORATEURS ====================
def permission_required(required_level):
    """Décorateur pour vérifier les permissions"""
    def decorator(f):
        @wraps(f)
        def decorated_function(*args, **kwargs):
            if 'user_id' not in session:
                return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non authentifié'}), 401
            user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
            if not user or not user.role or user.role.niveau < required_level:
                return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Permission insuffisante'}), 403
            return f(*args, **kwargs)
        return decorated_function
    return decorator


def log_action(action, entity_type, entity_id, details):
    """Journalisation des actions dans l'audit trail"""
    if 'user_id' in session:
        try:
            log = AuditLog(
                user_id=session['user_id'],
                action=action,
                entity_type=entity_type,
                entity_id=entity_id,
                details=details[:500] if details else '',
                ip_address=request.remote_addr
            )
            db.session.add(log)
            db.session.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erreur log: {e}")


def sanitize_input(text):
    """Nettoie les entrées utilisateur"""
    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
        return text
    import re
    text = re.sub(r'[<>]', '', text)
    return text.strip()[:500]


def generate_csrf_token():
    """Génère token CSRF"""
    import secrets
    if 'csrf_token' not in session:
        session['csrf_token'] = secrets.token_hex(32)
    return session['csrf_token']


def log_security_event(event_type, details):
    """Logge les événements de sécurité"""
    print(f"[SECURITY] {datetime.now()} - {event_type} - {details}")


# ==================== INITIALISATION DE LA BASE DE DONNÉES ====================
def init_database():
    """Initialise la base de données avec les rôles et utilisateurs par défaut"""
    with app.app_context():
        db.create_all()
        
        # Création des rôles
        roles_data = [
            {'nom': 'stagiaire', 'niveau': 1, 'description': 'Accès limité - consultation uniquement'},
            {'nom': 'assistant', 'niveau': 3, 'description': 'Peut scanner et ajouter des courriers'},
            {'nom': 'charge_etude', 'niveau': 5, 'description': 'Peut modifier et exporter les courriers'},
            {'nom': 'chef_service', 'niveau': 8, 'description': 'Peut supprimer et archiver'},
            {'nom': 'directeur', 'niveau': 10, 'description': 'Accès total à toutes les fonctionnalités'}
        ]
        
        for role_data in roles_data:
            if not Role.query.filter_by(nom=role_data['nom']).first():
                role = Role(**role_data)
                db.session.add(role)
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        # Création des utilisateurs de test
        test_users = [
            {'username': 'ahmed.directeur', 'password': 'directeur123', 'nom_complet': 'Ahmed Benali', 
             'email': 'ahmed@matn.gov.ma', 'role': 'directeur', 'service': 'Direction Générale', 'telephone': '0612345678'},
            {'username': 'fatima.chef', 'password': 'chef123', 'nom_complet': 'Fatima Zahra', 
             'email': 'fatima@urbanisme.gov.ma', 'role': 'chef_service', 'service': 'Urbanisme', 'telephone': '0623456789'},
            {'username': 'karim.assistant', 'password': 'assistant123', 'nom_complet': 'Karim Tazi', 
             'email': 'karim@habitat.gov.ma', 'role': 'assistant', 'service': 'Habitat', 'telephone': '0634567890'},
            {'username': 'nadia.etude', 'password': 'etude123', 'nom_complet': 'Nadia El Fassi', 
             'email': 'nadia@amenagement.gov.ma', 'role': 'charge_etude', 'service': 'Aménagement', 'telephone': '0645678901'},
            {'username': 'youssef.stagiaire', 'password': 'stagiaire123', 'nom_complet': 'Youssef Meriem', 
             'email': 'youssef@matn.gov.ma', 'role': 'stagiaire', 'service': 'Politique Ville', 'telephone': '0656789012'}
        ]
        
        for user_data in test_users:
            if not User.query.filter_by(username=user_data['username']).first():
                role = Role.query.filter_by(nom=user_data['role']).first()
                if role:
                    user = User(
                        username=user_data['username'],
                        nom_complet=user_data['nom_complet'],
                        email=user_data['email'],
                        telephone=user_data['telephone'],
                        role_id=role.id,
                        service=user_data['service'],
                        departement=user_data['service'],
                        statut='actif'
                    )
                    user.set_password(user_data['password'])
                    db.session.add(user)
        
        db.session.commit()
