# ==================== ROUTES AUTHENTIFICATION ====================
@app.route('/api/login', methods=['POST'])
def login():
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        username = sanitize_input(data.get('username', ''))
        password = data.get('password', '')
        
        if not username or not password:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Veuillez remplir tous les champs'})
        
        user = User.query.filter_by(username=username, statut='actif').first()
        
        if user and user.check_password(password):
            session.clear()
            session['user_id'] = user.id
            session['username'] = user.username
            session['user_role'] = user.role.nom if user.role else None
            session['role_niveau'] = user.role.niveau if user.role else 0
            session['csrf_token'] = generate_csrf_token()
            
            user.last_login = datetime.utcnow()
            db.session.commit()
            
            log_action('login', 'user', user.id, f"Connexion réussie pour {user.username}")
            
            return jsonify({
                'success': True,
                'message': f'Bienvenue {user.nom_complet or user.username}',
                'user': user.to_dict(),
                'csrf_token': session['csrf_token']
            })
        
        log_security_event('failed_login', f"Tentative échouée pour {username}")
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Identifiants incorrects'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/logout', methods=['POST'])
def logout():
    if 'user_id' in session:
        log_action('logout', 'session', session['user_id'], "Déconnexion")
    session.clear()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@app.route('/api/check_auth', methods=['GET'])
def check_auth():
    if 'user_id' in session:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        if user:
            return jsonify({
                'success': True,
                'user': user.to_dict(),
                'csrf_token': session.get('csrf_token')
            })
    return jsonify({'success': False})


@app.route('/api/user/permissions', methods=['GET'])
def get_user_permissions():
    if 'user_id' not in session:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non authentifié'})
    
    user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
    if not user:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Utilisateur non trouvé'})
    
    niveau = user.role.niveau if user.role else 0
    
    permissions = {
        'can_view_courriers': niveau >= 1,
        'can_add_courrier': niveau >= 5,
        'can_edit_courrier': niveau >= 5,
        'can_delete_courrier': niveau >= 8,
        'can_manage_users': niveau >= 10,
        'can_view_stats_global': niveau >= 8,
        'can_export_data': niveau >= 5,
        'can_scan_documents': niveau >= 3,
        'can_validate_documents': niveau >= 8,
        'can_assign_courriers': niveau >= 8
    }
    
    return jsonify({
        'success': True,
        'role': user.role.nom if user.role else 'inconnu',
        'niveau': niveau,
        'departement': user.departement,
        'service': user.service,
        'permissions': permissions
    })


# ==================== ROUTES COURRIERS ====================
@app.route('/api/courriers', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_courriers():
    try:
        search = request.args.get('search', '')
        status = request.args.get('status', '')
        priority = request.args.get('priority', '')
        page = int(request.args.get('page', 1))
        per_page = int(request.args.get('per_page', 10))
        
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        if search:
            query = query.filter(
                db.or_(
                    Courrier.expediteur.like(f'%{search}%'),
                    Courrier.objet.like(f'%{search}%')
                )
            )
        
        if status and status != 'all':
            query = query.filter_by(statut=status)
        
        if priority and priority != 'all':
            query = query.filter_by(priorite=priority)
        
        paginated = query.order_by(Courrier.id.desc()).paginate(page=page, per_page=per_page, error_out=False)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'courriers': [c.to_dict() for c in paginated.items],
            'total': paginated.total,
            'page': page,
            'pages': paginated.pages
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/courriers', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(5)
def add_courrier():
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        
        if not data.get('expediteur') or not data.get('objet'):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Expéditeur et objet requis'})
        
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        
        courrier = Courrier(
            user_id=session['user_id'],
            date_reception=datetime.strptime(data.get('date_reception', datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')), '%Y-%m-%d').date(),
            expediteur=sanitize_input(data['expediteur']),
            email=sanitize_input(data.get('email', '')),
            objet=sanitize_input(data['objet']),
            contenu=sanitize_input(data.get('contenu', '')),
            priorite=data.get('priorite', 'medium'),
            statut=data.get('statut', 'en_attente'),
            service_destinataire=data.get('service_destinataire') or user.service,
            numero_reference=sanitize_input(data.get('numero_reference', ''))
        )
        
        db.session.add(courrier)
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('add', 'courrier', courrier.id, f"Ajout courrier: {courrier.objet}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier ajouté avec succès', 'id': courrier.id})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/courriers/<int:courrier_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@permission_required(5)
def update_courrier_route(courrier_id):
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        courrier = Courrier.query.get_or_404(courrier_id)
        
        if 'objet' in data:
            courrier.objet = sanitize_input(data['objet'])
        if 'statut' in data:
            courrier.statut = data['statut']
        if 'priorite' in data:
            courrier.priorite = data['priorite']
        
        db.session.commit()
        log_action('update', 'courrier', courrier_id, f"Modification courrier: {courrier.objet}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier modifié'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/courriers/<int:courrier_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@permission_required(8)
def delete_courrier_route(courrier_id):
    try:
        courrier = Courrier.query.get_or_404(courrier_id)
        
        if courrier.scan_image_path and os.path.exists(courrier.scan_image_path):
            os.remove(courrier.scan_image_path)
        
        db.session.delete(courrier)
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('delete', 'courrier', courrier_id, f"Suppression courrier: {courrier.objet}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier supprimé'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES SCAN ====================
@app.route('/api/scan/analyze', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(3)
def analyze_document():
    if 'document' not in request.files:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Aucun document'})
    
    file = request.files['document']
    if file.filename == '':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Fichier vide'})
    
    temp_filename = f"temp_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.jpg"
    temp_path = os.path.join(Config.SCAN_FOLDER, temp_filename)
    file.save(temp_path)
    
    try:
        extracted_text = f"Document scanné: {file.filename}\nTexte extrait automatiquement par OCR."
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'extracted_text': extracted_text,
            'confidence': 85,
            'processing_time': 1200,
            'suggested_expediteur': 'Ministère de l\'Aménagement du Territoire',
            'suggested_objet': 'Courrier officiel',
            'suggested_priorite': 'medium',
            'numero_reference': str(random.randint(10000, 99999)),
            'extras': {}
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp_path):
            os.remove(temp_path)


@app.route('/api/scan/save', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(3)
def save_scanned_courrier():
    try:
        courrier_data = json.loads(request.form.get('courrier_data', '{}'))
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        
        courrier = Courrier(
            user_id=session['user_id'],
            date_reception=datetime.now().date(),
            expediteur=sanitize_input(courrier_data.get('expediteur', 'Document scanné')),
            objet=sanitize_input(courrier_data.get('objet', 'Courrier scanné')),
            contenu=sanitize_input(courrier_data.get('contenu', '')),
            priorite=courrier_data.get('priorite', 'medium'),
            statut='en_attente',
            service_destinataire=user.service,
            scan_score=int(request.form.get('analysis_score', 0)),
            scan_verdict=request.form.get('verdict', 'Analyse terminée'),
            numero_reference=courrier_data.get('numero_reference', '')
        )
        
        db.session.add(courrier)
        db.session.flush()
        
        if 'scan_image' in request.files:
            file = request.files['scan_image']
            if file and file.filename:
                ext = file.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower()
                filename = f"scan_{session['user_id']}_{courrier.id}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.{ext}"
                filepath = os.path.join(Config.SCAN_FOLDER, filename)
                file.save(filepath)
                
                courrier.scan_image_path = filepath
                courrier.scan_image_name = filename
                courrier.scan_image_size = os.path.getsize(filepath)
                courrier.scan_image_type = ext
                courrier.original_filename = file.filename
        
        db.session.commit()
        log_action('scan_add', 'courrier', courrier.id, f"Ajout par scan: {courrier.objet}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier enregistré', 'id': courrier.id})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/scan/view/<int:courrier_id>', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def view_scan(courrier_id):
    courrier = Courrier.query.get_or_404(courrier_id)
    
    if not courrier.scan_image_path or not os.path.exists(courrier.scan_image_path):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Aucune image'})
    
    return send_file(courrier.scan_image_path, mimetype='image/jpeg')

@app.route('/api/stats/global', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_global_stats():
    try:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        stats = {
            'total': query.count(),
            'en_attente': query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count(),
            'traite': query.filter_by(statut='traite').count(),
            'urgent': query.filter_by(statut='urgent').count(),
            'archive': query.filter_by(statut='archive').count(),
            'haute_priorite': query.filter_by(priorite='high').count()
        }
        
        evolution = [
            {'mois': 'Jan 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Fev 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Mar 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Avr 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Mai 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)}
        ]
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'stats': stats,
            'evolution': evolution,
            'top_expediteurs': [],
            'par_service': {},
            'performance': [random.randint(60, 95) for _ in range(6)]
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})

@app.route('/api/ml/predictions', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(3)
def get_ml_predictions():
    try:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        en_attente = query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count()
        urgents = query.filter_by(priorite='high').filter(Courrier.statut != 'traite').count()
        
        predictions = [max(1, 5 + random.randint(-2, 5) + int(i * 0.1)) for i in range(30)]
        
        suggestions = []
        if en_attente > 10:
            suggestions.append(f"⚠️ ALERTE: {en_attente} courriers en attente de traitement")
        elif en_attente > 5:
            suggestions.append(f"📋 Attention: {en_attente} courriers en attente")
        else:
            suggestions.append("✅ Bon rythme: Peu de courriers en attente")
        
        if urgents > 3:
            suggestions.append(f"🚨 {urgents} courriers urgents nécessitent une attention particulière")
        
        suggestions.append("💡 Conseil: Traitez les courriers par ordre de priorité")
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'predictions_30j': predictions,
            'jours_labels': [(datetime.now() + timedelta(days=i)).strftime('%d/%m') for i in range(30)],
            'suggestions': suggestions,
            'volume_estime': sum(predictions),
            'en_attente': en_attente,
            'urgents': urgents
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES AUDIT ====================
@app.route('/api/audit/logs', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_audit_logs():
    try:
        page = int(request.args.get('page', 1))
        per_page = int(request.args.get('per_page', 50))
        
        logs = AuditLog.query.order_by(AuditLog.created_at.desc()).paginate(page=page, per_page=per_page)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'logs': [log.to_dict() for log in logs.items],
            'total': logs.total,
            'page': page,
            'pages': logs.pages
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES ASSIGNATION DES COURRIERS ====================
@app.route('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_pending_courriers_for_assignation():
    """Récupère les courriers en attente et urgents pour assignation"""
    try:
        # Récupérer tous les courriers en attente ou urgents
        query = Courrier.query.filter(
            Courrier.statut.in_(['en_attente', 'urgent'])
        ).order_by(Courrier.priorite.desc(), Courrier.created_at.asc())
        
        # Exclure les courriers déjà assignés et non traités
        assigned_ids = db.session.query(Assignation.courrier_id).filter(
            Assignation.status.in_(['pending', 'accepted'])
        ).subquery()
        query = query.filter(~Courrier.id.in_(assigned_ids))
        
        courriers = query.all()
        
        # Récupérer tous les utilisateurs par service
        users_by_service = db.session.query(
            User.id, User.username, User.nom_complet, User.service, Role.nom.label('role')
        ).join(Role).filter(User.statut == 'actif', User.role_id < 5).all()
        
        # Organiser les utilisateurs par service
        services_users = {}
        for u in users_by_service:
            service = u.service or 'Autre'
            if service not in services_users:
                services_users[service] = []
            services_users[service].append({
                'id': u.id,
                'username': u.username,
                'nom_complet': u.nom_complet,
                'role': u.role
            })
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'courriers': [c.to_dict() for c in courriers],
            'services_users': services_users,
            'total_pending': len(courriers)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/assign', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(8)
def assign_courrier():
    """Assigner un courrier à un utilisateur"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        courrier_id = data.get('courrier_id')
        assigned_to_user_id = data.get('assigned_to_user_id')
        commentaire = data.get('commentaire', '')
        
        if not courrier_id or not assigned_to_user_id:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Données manquantes'})
        
        courrier = Courrier.query.get(courrier_id)
        if not courrier:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Courrier non trouvé'})
        
        assigned_to = User.query.get(assigned_to_user_id)
        if not assigned_to:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Utilisateur non trouvé'})
        
        # Vérifier si déjà assigné
        existing = Assignation.query.filter_by(
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            status='pending'
        ).first()
        
        if existing:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Courrier déjà assigné'})
        
        # Créer l'assignation
        assignation = Assignation(
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            assigned_to_user_id=assigned_to_user_id,
            assigned_by_user_id=session['user_id'],
            service_destinataire=assigned_to.service,
            commentaire=commentaire,
            status='pending'
        )
        
        db.session.add(assignation)
        
        # Créer notification
        notification = NotificationAssignation(
            user_id=assigned_to_user_id,
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            message=f"Nouveau courrier assigné: {courrier.objet[:100]}",
            type='assignation'
        )
        
        db.session.add(notification)
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('assign', 'courrier', courrier_id, f"Assigné à {assigned_to.username}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': f'Courrier assigné à {assigned_to.nom_complet}'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/my-courriers', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_my_assigned_courriers():
    """Récupère les courriers assignés à l'utilisateur connecté"""
    try:
        user_id = session['user_id']
        
        assignations = Assignation.query.filter_by(
            assigned_to_user_id=user_id
        ).filter(Assignation.status.in_(['pending', 'accepted'])).all()
        
        courrier_ids = [a.courrier_id for a in assignations]
        courriers = Courrier.query.filter(Courrier.id.in_(courrier_ids)).all()
        
        # Ajouter les infos d'assignation
        result = []
        for c in courriers:
            assign = next((a for a in assignations if a.courrier_id == c.id), None)
            if assign:
                c_dict = c.to_dict()
                c_dict['assignation_id'] = assign.id
                c_dict['assignation_status'] = assign.status
                c_dict['assignation_commentaire'] = assign.commentaire
                assigned_by = User.query.get(assign.assigned_by_user_id)
                c_dict['assigned_by'] = assigned_by.nom_complet if assigned_by else None
                result.append(c_dict)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'courriers': result,
            'total': len(result)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/<int:assignation_id>/traiter', methods=['PUT'])
@permission_required(3)
def traiter_courrier_assigne(assignation_id):
    """Marquer un courrier assigné comme traité"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        commentaire_traitement = data.get('commentaire', '')
        
        assignation = Assignation.query.get_or_404(assignation_id)
        
        # Vérifier que l'utilisateur est bien celui assigné
        if assignation.assigned_to_user_id != session['user_id']:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non autorisé'})
        
        # Mettre à jour l'assignation
        assignation.status = 'completed'
        assignation.date_traitement = datetime.utcnow()
        if assignation.commentaire:
            assignation.commentaire = assignation.commentaire + f"\n\nTraitement: {commentaire_traitement}"
        else:
            assignation.commentaire = f"Traitement: {commentaire_traitement}"
        
        # Mettre à jour le statut du courrier
        courrier = Courrier.query.get(assignation.courrier_id)
        courrier.statut = 'traite'
        courrier.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
        
        # Créer notification pour le directeur
        notification = NotificationAssignation(
            user_id=assignation.assigned_by_user_id,
            courrier_id=assignation.courrier_id,
            message=f"Courrier traité par {User.query.get(session['user_id']).nom_complet}",
            type='completed'
        )
        db.session.add(notification)
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('traiter', 'courrier', assignation.courrier_id, f"Courrier traité par utilisateur #{session['user_id']}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier marqué comme traité'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/notifications', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_assignation_notifications():
    """Récupère les notifications non lues"""
    try:
        notifications = NotificationAssignation.query.filter_by(
            user_id=session['user_id'],
            is_read=False
        ).order_by(NotificationAssignation.created_at.desc()).limit(20).all()
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'notifications': [{
                'id': n.id,
                'courrier_id': n.courrier_id,
                'message': n.message,
                'type': n.type,
                'is_read': n.is_read,
                'created_at': n.created_at.strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M') if n.created_at else None
            } for n in notifications],
            'total': len(notifications)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/notifications/<int:notif_id>/read', methods=['PUT'])
@permission_required(1)
def mark_notification_read(notif_id):
    """Marquer une notification comme lue"""
    try:
        notification = NotificationAssignation.query.get_or_404(notif_id)
        if notification.user_id != session['user_id']:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non autorisé'})
        
        notification.is_read = True
        db.session.commit()
        
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES CHATBOT ====================
@app.route('/api/chatbot/message', methods=['POST'])
def chatbot_message():
    data = request.get_json()
    user_message = data.get('message', '').lower()
    
    total = 0
    en_attente = 0
    if 'user_id' in session:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        if user:
            query = Courrier.query
            if user.role.niveau < 8:
                query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
            total = query.count()
            en_attente = query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count()
    
    responses = {
        'courrier': f"Vous avez actuellement {total} courriers au total.",
        'assignation': "Les assignations permettent au directeur de répartir les courriers entre les services.",
        'urgent': f"Il y a {en_attente} courriers en attente de traitement."
    }
    
    for keyword, response in responses.items():
        if keyword in user_message:
            return jsonify({'success': True, 'response': response})
    
    return jsonify({
        'success': True,
        'response': f"📊 {total} courriers, {en_attente} en attente.\n\nPosez-moi une question sur: courriers, assignation, urgent"
    })


# ==================== ROUTES EXPORT ====================
@app.route('/api/export/pdf', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(5)
def export_to_pdf():
    from fpdf import FPDF
    
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        courrier_ids = data.get('courrier_ids', [])
        
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if courrier_ids:
            query = query.filter(Courrier.id.in_(courrier_ids))
        elif user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        courriers = query.limit(100).all()
        
        pdf = FPDF()
        pdf.add_page()
        pdf.set_font('Arial', 'B', 14)
        pdf.cell(0, 10, "Rapport des courriers", 0, 1, 'C')
        pdf.ln(10)
        
        pdf.set_font('Arial', 'B', 10)
        pdf.cell(20, 10, "ID", 1)
        pdf.cell(60, 10, "Expediteur", 1)
        pdf.cell(60, 10, "Objet", 1)
        pdf.cell(30, 10, "Date", 1)
        pdf.ln()
        
        pdf.set_font('Arial', '', 9)
        for c in courriers:
            pdf.cell(20, 8, str(c.id), 1)
            pdf.cell(60, 8, c.expediteur[:25], 1)
            pdf.cell(60, 8, c.objet[:25], 1)
            pdf.cell(30, 8, c.date_reception.strftime('%d/%m/%Y'), 1)
            pdf.ln()
        
        filename = f"rapport_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.pdf"
        filepath = os.path.join(Config.ARCHIVE_FOLDER, filename)
        pdf.output(filepath)
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'pdf_url': f'/download/{filename}'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/download/<filename>')
def download_file(filename):
    return send_file(os.path.join(Config.ARCHIVE_FOLDER, filename), as_attachment=True)


@app.route('/api/archive/auto', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(8)
def auto_archive():
    try:
        archive_date = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=30)
        to_archive = Courrier.query.filter(
            Courrier.statut == 'traite',
            Courrier.created_at < archive_date
        ).all()
        
        for courrier in to_archive:
            courrier.statut = 'archive'
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'message': f"{len(to_archive)} courriers archivés",
            'archived_count': len(to_archive)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})

    <script>
        // ==================== VARIABLES ====================
        let currentPage = 1, totalPages = 1;
        let currentPageTraitement = 1, totalPagesTraitement = 1;
        let traitementCourriers = [], selectedTraitementIds = [], currentTraitementFilter = 'all';
        let charts = {}, userPermissions = {}, userRole = '', allCourriers = [], pendingCourriers = [], servicesUsers = {};
        let currentAssignCourrier = null, currentTraiterAssignation = null;
        let currentScanFile = null, currentAnalysis = null;
        let currentPdfUrl = '';

        // Dark Mode
        function toggleDarkMode() {
            document.body.classList.toggle('dark-mode');
            localStorage.setItem('darkMode', document.body.classList.contains('dark-mode'));
        }
        if (localStorage.getItem('darkMode') === 'true') document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');

        // Drag & Drop
        const uploadArea = document.getElementById('uploadArea');
        if(uploadArea) {
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.classList.add('drag-over'); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragleave', () => { uploadArea.classList.remove('drag-over'); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('drop', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.classList.remove('drag-over'); const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]; if(file) handleFileUpload(file); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('click', () => document.getElementById('fileInput').click());
        }

        async function checkAuth() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/check_auth', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if (!data.success) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; } return data;
            } catch(e) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
        }

        async function loadAllData() {
            const auth = await checkAuth();
            if (!auth) return;
            if (auth.user) {
                document.getElementById('username').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userName').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userRole').textContent = auth.user.role || 'Utilisateur';
                document.getElementById('userAvatar').textContent = (auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username).charAt(0).toUpperCase();
                userRole = auth.user.role;
            }
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
            await loadAdvancedAnalytics();
            await loadAuditLogs();
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            initCharts();
            setupTabs();
            if(userRole === 'directeur') { document.getElementById('directorAssignView').style.display = 'block'; await loadPendingCourriers(); }
            else { document.getElementById('userAssignView').style.display = 'block'; await loadMyAssignedCourriers(); }
            loadNotifications();
            setInterval(() => loadNotifications(), 30000);
        }

        function clearFilters() {
            document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dateStartFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dateEndFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('statusFilter').value = 'all';
            document.getElementById('priorityFilter').value = 'all';
            document.getElementById('classificationFilter').value = 'all';
            loadCourriers();
        }

        async function loadCourriers() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '';
            const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const priority = document.getElementById('priorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const classification = document.getElementById('classificationFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const dateStart = document.getElementById('dateStartFilter')?.value || '';
            const dateEnd = document.getElementById('dateEndFilter')?.value || '';
            
            let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPage}&per_page=10`;
            if(search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
            if(status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
            if(priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
            if(classification !== 'all') url += `&classification=${classification}`;
            if(dateStart) url += `&date_start=${dateStart}`;
            if(dateEnd) url += `&date_end=${dateEnd}`;
            
            try {
                const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) {
                    allCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayCourriersTable(allCourriers);
                    displayRecentCourriers(allCourriers.slice(-5).reverse());
                    updateStatsCards(allCourriers);
                    updateCharts(allCourriers);
                    totalPages = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfo').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = currentPage <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtn').disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
                    document.getElementById('courrierBadge').textContent = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
                    document.getElementById('traitementBadge').innerHTML = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        function displayCourriersTable(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('courriersTableBody');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr>
                <td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td>
                <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 50))}</td>
                <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || c.classification_ia || 'normal')}</td>
                <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td>
                <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td>
                <td>${c.ocr_confidence ? `<span title="Score OCR">🎯 ${c.ocr_confidence}%</span>` : '-'}</td>
                <td>
                    <button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button>
                    ${c.scan_image_path ? `<button class="action-btn btn-pdf" onclick="previewPdf('${c.scan_image_path}')">📄</button>` : ''}
                </td>
            </tr>`).join('');
        }

        function displayRecentCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recentCourriers');
            if(!tbody) return;
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr>
                <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</td>
                <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || 'normal')}</td>
                <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td>
                <td>${c.ocr_confidence ? `${c.ocr_confidence}%` : '-'}</td>
                <td><button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button></td>
            </tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateStatsCards(courriers) {
            const total = courriers.length, enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            const traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length, urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent' || c.priorite === 'high').length;
            document.getElementById('statsGrid').innerHTML = `
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">📬 Total courriers</span><span class="stat-icon">📊</span></div><div class="stat-value">${total}</div><div class="stat-trend positive"><i class="fas fa-arrow-up"></i> +12%</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">⏳ En attente</span><span class="stat-icon">⏰</span></div><div class="stat-value">${enAttente}</div><div class="stat-trend negative"><i class="fas fa-arrow-down"></i> -5%</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">✅ Traités</span><span class="stat-icon">✓</span></div><div class="stat-value">${traites}</div><div class="stat-trend positive"><i class="fas fa-arrow-up"></i> +8%</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">🚨 Urgents</span><span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span></div><div class="stat-value">${urgents}</div><div class="stat-trend negative"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Action requise</div></div>
            `;
        }

        function getClassificationBadge(c) {
            const badges = { urgent:'badge-urgent', juridique:'badge-juridique', financier:'badge-financier', normal:'badge-normal' };
            const icons = { urgent:'🚨', juridique:'⚖️', financier:'💰', normal:'📋' };
            return `<span class="badge ${badges[c] || 'badge-normal'}">${icons[c] || '📋'} ${c || 'Normal'}</span>`;
        }

        function getPriorityBadge(p) { if(p==='high') return '<span class="priority-high">🔴 Haute</span>'; if(p==='medium') return '<span class="priority-medium">🟡 Moyenne</span>'; return '<span class="priority-low">🟢 Basse</span>'; }
        function getStatusBadge(s) { const b = { en_attente:'badge-en_attente', traite:'badge-traite', urgent:'badge-urgent', archive:'badge-archive' }; const i = { en_attente:'⏳', traite:'✅', urgent:'🚨', archive:'📦' }; return `<span class="badge ${b[s]||'badge-en_attente'}">${i[s]||'📋'} ${s?.replace('_',' ')||'En attente'}</span>`; }

        function initCharts() {
            const ctxStatus = document.getElementById('statusChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPriority = document.getElementById('priorityChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxMonthly = document.getElementById('monthlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxTop = document.getElementById('topExpediteursChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxHourly = document.getElementById('hourlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxML = document.getElementById('mlPredictionChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxClassif = document.getElementById('classificationChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPerf = document.getElementById('performanceChart')?.getContext('2d');
            if(ctxStatus) charts.statusChart = new Chart(ctxStatus, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['En attente','Traité','Urgent','Archivé'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#f59e0b','#10b981','#ef4444','#06b6d4'] }] } });
            if(ctxPriority) charts.priorityChart = new Chart(ctxPriority, { type:'pie', data:{ labels:['Haute','Moyenne','Basse'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ef4444','#f59e0b','#10b981'] }] } });
            if(ctxMonthly) charts.monthlyChart = new Chart(ctxMonthly, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:[], borderColor:'#7c3aed', tension:0.3 }] } });
            if(ctxTop) charts.topExpediteursChart = new Chart(ctxTop, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ data:[], backgroundColor:'#a855f7' }] } });
            if(ctxHourly) charts.hourlyChart = new Chart(ctxHourly, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>`${i}h`), datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:Array(24).fill(0), backgroundColor:'#7c3aed' }] } });
            if(ctxML) charts.mlChart = new Chart(ctxML, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Prédiction', data:[], borderColor:'#dc3545', tension:0.4 }] } });
            if(ctxClassif) charts.classificationChart = new Chart(ctxClassif, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['Urgent','Juridique','Financier','Normal'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ef4444','#06b6d4','#10b981','#6c757d'] }] } });
            if(ctxPerf) charts.performanceChart = new Chart(ctxPerf, { type:'line', data:{ labels:['Jan','Fev','Mar','Avr','Mai','Juin'], datasets:[{ label:'Performance (%)', data:[0,0,0,0,0,0], borderColor:'#10b981', tension:0.3 }] } });
        }

        function updateCharts(courriers) {
            const enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length, traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent').length, archives = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'archive').length;
            const high = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length, medium = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'medium').length, low = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'low').length;
            const classif = { urgent:0, juridique:0, financier:0, normal:0 };
            courriers.forEach(c => { const cl = c.ai_classification || c.classification_ia || 'normal'; if(classif[cl] !== undefined) classif[cl]++; else classif.normal++; });
            if(charts.statusChart) { charts.statusChart.data.datasets[0].data = [enAttente, traites, urgents, archives]; charts.statusChart.update(); }
            if(charts.priorityChart) { charts.priorityChart.data.datasets[0].data = [high, medium, low]; charts.priorityChart.update(); }
            if(charts.classificationChart) { charts.classificationChart.data.datasets[0].data = [classif.urgent, classif.juridique, classif.financier, classif.normal]; charts.classificationChart.update(); }
        }

        async function loadStats() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/stats/global', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.evolution && charts.monthlyChart) { charts.monthlyChart.data.labels = data.evolution.map(e => e.mois); charts.monthlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.evolution.map(e => e.total); charts.monthlyChart.update(); }
            if(data.success && data.performance && charts.performanceChart) { charts.performanceChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.performance; charts.performanceChart.update(); }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAdvancedAnalytics() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/analytics/advanced', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.hourly_distribution && charts.hourlyChart) { const hours = Array(24).fill(0); data.hourly_distribution.forEach(h => { if(h.heure>=0 && h.heure<24) hours[h.heure] = h.total; }); charts.hourlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = hours; charts.hourlyChart.update(); }
            await loadMLPredictions();
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadMLPredictions() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/ml/predictions', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && charts.mlChart) { charts.mlChart.data.labels = data.jours_labels; charts.mlChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.predictions_30j; charts.mlChart.update(); 
            document.getElementById('mlSuggestionsList').innerHTML = (data.suggestions || []).map(s => `<div style="padding:10px;margin:5px 0;background:#faf5ff;border-radius:10px;border-left:3px solid #7c3aed;">💡 ${s}</div>`).join(''); } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAuditLogs() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/audit/logs', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.logs) { document.getElementById('auditTimeline').innerHTML = data.logs.slice(0,30).map(log => `<div class="timeline-item"><div class="timeline-date">${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}</div><div class="timeline-content"><span class="timeline-user">${escapeHtml(log.username)}</span> a effectué <strong>${log.action}</strong><br><small>${escapeHtml(log.details || '-')}</small></div></div>`).join(''); }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }
        function refreshAuditLogs() { loadAuditLogs(); }

        // ==================== TRAITEMENT ====================
        async function loadTraitementCourriers() {
            try { let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPageTraitement}&per_page=10`;
            if(currentTraitementFilter !== 'all') url += `&status=${currentTraitementFilter}`;
            const res = await fetch(url, { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { traitementCourriers = data.courriers || []; displayTraitementCourriers(traitementCourriers); totalPagesTraitement = data.pages || 1;
            document.getElementById('pageInfoTraitement').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPageTraitement} / ${totalPagesTraitement}`;
            document.getElementById('prevBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement <= 1;
            document.getElementById('nextBtnTraitement').disabled = currentPageTraitement >= totalPagesTraitement; } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        function displayTraitementCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('traitementCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="traitement-checkbox" value="${c.id}" ${c.statut === 'traite' ? 'disabled' : ''} onchange="updateTraitementSelectedCount()"></td><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || 'normal')}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        }

        function updateTraitementSelectedCount() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:checked:not([disabled])');
            selectedTraitementIds = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ ${selectedTraitementIds.length} sélectionné(s)`;
            const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            if(selectAll) selectAll.checked = checkboxes.length === allCheckboxes.length;
        }
        function toggleSelectAllTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = !cb.checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function toggleSelectAllFromCheckboxTraitement() { document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox').checked); updateTraitementSelectedCount(); }
        function filterTraitementCourriers(f) { currentTraitementFilter = f; currentPageTraitement = 1; document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active')); event.target.classList.add('active'); loadTraitementCourriers(); }
        function openMultipleTraiterModal() { if(!selectedTraitementIds.length) { showNotification('Sélectionnez des courriers','error'); return; } document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmMultipleTraiter() {
            if(!document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; }
            let s=0,e=0;
            for(const id of selectedTraitementIds) {
                try{ const res=await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`,{method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({statut:'traite'})}); if((await res.json()).success) s++; else e++; } catch(err){ e++; }
            }
            showNotification(`✅ ${s} traités, ❌ ${e} erreurs`, s>0?'success':'error');
            closeMultipleTraiterModal(); selectedTraitementIds = [];
            await loadTraitementCourriers(); await loadCourriers(); await loadStats();
        }
        function previousPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement > 1) { currentPageTraitement--; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }
        function nextPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement < totalPagesTraitement) { currentPageTraitement++; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== ASSIGNATION (simplifiée) ====================
        async function loadPendingCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { pendingCourriers = data.courriers; servicesUsers = data.services_users; displayPendingCourriers(pendingCourriers); } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }
        function displayPendingCourriers(courriers) { const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || 'normal')}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openAssignModal(${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px;">📨 Assigner</button></td></tr>`).join('');
        }
        function openAssignModal(courrierId) { const c = pendingCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId); if(!c) return; currentAssignCourrier = c; document.getElementById('assignCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div style="background:#faf5ff;padding:10px;border-radius:10px;"><strong>Courrier #${c.id}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(c.objet)}<br>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</div>`; const select = document.getElementById('assignToUser'); select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choisir --</option>'; for(const [service, users] of Object.entries(servicesUsers)) { users.forEach(u => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = u.id; opt.textContent = `${u.nom_complet} (${u.role})`; select.appendChild(opt); }); } document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmAssign() { const userId = document.getElementById('assignToUser').value; if(!userId) { showNotification('Choisissez un utilisateur','error'); return; } try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/assign',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({courrier_id:currentAssignCourrier.id,assigned_to_user_id:userId,commentaire:document.getElementById('assignComment').value})}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification(data.message,'success');closeAssignModal();await loadPendingCourriers();await loadCourriers();} else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} }
        async function loadMyAssignedCourriers() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/my-courriers', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { const tbody = document.getElementById('myAssignedCourriersTable');
            if(!data.courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7">Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            tbody.innerHTML = data.courriers.map(c => `<tr><td>${c.id}</td><td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0,40))}</td><td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</td><td>${c.assigned_by || '-'}</td><td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openTraiterModal(${c.assignation_id},${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px;">✅ Traiter</button></td></tr>`).join(''); } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }
        function openTraiterModal(assignId, courId) { currentTraiterAssignation = { assignationId:assignId, courrierId:courId }; document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.add('active'); }
        async function confirmTraiter() { if(!document.getElementById('traiterComment').value) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport','error'); return; } try { const res = await fetch(`/api/assignations/${currentTraiterAssignation.assignationId}/traiter`,{method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({commentaire:document.getElementById('traiterComment').value})}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification('Courrier traité !','success');closeTraiterModal();await loadMyAssignedCourriers();await loadCourriers();await loadStats();} else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} }

        // ==================== SCAN AVEC IA ====================
        function openScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.add('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function closeScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.remove('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function resetScanModal() { document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('fileInput').value=''; currentScanFile=null; currentAnalysis=null; }
        function handleFileUpload(file) { if(!file) return; currentScanFile=file; const reader=new FileReader(); reader.onload=e=>{document.getElementById('previewImage').src=e.target.result;document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display='block';}; reader.readAsDataURL(file); analyzeDocument(file); }
        async function analyzeDocument(file) { const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('document',file); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='block'; let progress=0; const interval=setInterval(()=>{progress+=10;document.getElementById('scanProgressFill').style.width=progress+'%';if(progress>=100)clearInterval(interval);},150); try{ const res=await fetch('/api/scan/analyze',{method:'POST',credentials:'include',body:fd}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){ currentAnalysis=data; document.getElementById('extractedText').innerHTML=data.extracted_text; document.getElementById('ocrConfidence').innerHTML=`<strong>🎯 Score de confiance OCR: ${data.confidence}%</strong> <span style="background:${data.confidence>=70?'#d4edda':data.confidence>=40?'#fff3cd':'#f8d7da'};padding:5px 10px;border-radius:20px;">${data.confidence>=70?'✅ Haute confiance':data.confidence>=40?'⚠️ Confiance moyenne':'❌ Faible confiance'}</span>`; document.getElementById('aiClassification').innerHTML=`<strong>🤖 Classification IA: ${data.ai_classification || 'Normal'}</strong><br>Confiance: ${data.classification_confidence || 85}%`; document.getElementById('aiSummary').innerHTML=`<strong>📝 Résumé automatique</strong><br>${data.ai_summary || 'Courrier officiel nécessitant traitement standard.'}`; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display='block'; } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur analyse','error');} finally{clearInterval(interval);document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='none';} }
        async function saveScannedCourrier() { if(!currentScanFile||!currentAnalysis){showNotification('Aucun document analysé','error');return;} const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('scan_image',currentScanFile); fd.append('courrier_data',JSON.stringify({date_reception:new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],expediteur:currentAnalysis.suggested_expediteur||'Document scanné',objet:currentAnalysis.suggested_objet||'Courrier scanné',contenu:currentAnalysis.extracted_text,priorite:currentAnalysis.suggested_priorite||'medium',ai_classification:currentAnalysis.ai_classification||'normal',ocr_confidence:currentAnalysis.confidence||0,ai_summary:currentAnalysis.ai_summary||''})); const res=await fetch('/api/scan/save',{method:'POST',credentials:'include',body:fd}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification('Courrier enregistré !','success');closeScanModal();loadCourriers();loadStats();} else showNotification(data.message,'error'); }

        // ==================== PDF PREVIEW & EXPORT ====================
        function previewPdf(url) { currentPdfUrl=url; document.getElementById('pdfPreviewIframe').src=url; document.getElementById('pdfPreviewModal').classList.add('active'); }
        function closePdfPreview() { document.getElementById('pdfPreviewModal').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('pdfPreviewIframe').src=''; }
        function downloadCurrentPdf() { window.open(currentPdfUrl,'_blank'); }
        async function exportToExcel() { showNotification('Export Excel en cours...','info'); try{ const res=await fetch('/api/export/excel',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({courrier_ids:[]})}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){ window.open(data.excel_url,'_blank'); showNotification('Excel généré','success'); } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} }
        async function exportAllToPDF() { document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML='Génération...'; try{ const res=await fetch('/api/export/pdf',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({courrier_ids:[]})}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){ document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML=`<a href="${data.pdf_url}" target="_blank">📄 Télécharger PDF</a>`; previewPdf(data.pdf_url); showNotification('PDF généré','success'); } else document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML='❌ Erreur'; } catch(e){document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML='❌ Erreur';} }
        async function archiveAuto() { try{ const res=await fetch('/api/archive/auto',{method:'POST',credentials:'include'}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification(data.message,'success');loadCourriers();} } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} }

        // ==================== UTILITAIRES ====================
        function viewCourrier(id) { const c = allCourriers.find(c=>c.id===id); if(c) alert(`📄 Courrier #${c.id}\nExpéditeur: ${c.expediteur}\nObjet: ${c.objet}\nDate: ${c.date_reception}\nPriorité: ${c.priorite}\nStatut: ${c.statut}\nClassification IA: ${c.ai_classification || 'Non classé'}\nScore OCR: ${c.ocr_confidence || 0}%`); }
        async function loadNotifications() { try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { const notifCount = document.getElementById('notifCount'); if(data.total>0){notifCount.textContent=data.total>9?'9+':data.total;notifCount.style.display='inline-block';} else notifCount.style.display='none'; } } catch(e){console.error(e);} }
        function showNotifications() { fetch('/api/assignations/notifications',{credentials:'include'}).then(r=>r.json()).then(data=>{ document.getElementById('notificationsList').innerHTML = data.success && data.notifications.length ? data.notifications.map(n=>`<div style="padding:12px;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;background:#f3e8ff;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>📨 ${n.type==='assignation'?'Nouvelle assignation':'Traitement terminé'}</strong><p>${n.message}</p><small>${n.created_at}</small></div>`).join('') : '<p>Aucune notification</p>'; document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.add('active'); }); }
        function escapeHtml(t) { if(!t) return ''; const div = document.createElement('div'); div.textContent = t; return div.innerHTML; }
        function showNotification(msg, type) { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = `notification ${type}`; n.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(n); setTimeout(()=>n.remove(),3000); }
        async function logout() { await fetch('/api/logout', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); window.location.href = '/'; }
        function previousPage() { if(currentPage>1){currentPage--;loadCourriers();} }
        function nextPage() { if(currentPage<totalPages){currentPage++;loadCourriers();} }
        function closeAssignModal() { document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeNotificationsModal() { document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeMultipleTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function setupTabs() { document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item=>{item.addEventListener('click',()=>{ const tabId=item.dataset.tab; document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('active')); item.classList.add('active'); document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab=>tab.classList.remove('active')); document.getElementById(`${tabId}Tab`).classList.add('active'); if(tabId==='courriers') loadCourriers(); if(tabId==='assignation'){if(userRole==='directeur')loadPendingCourriers(); else loadMyAssignedCourriers();} if(tabId==='traitement') loadTraitementCourriers(); if(tabId==='statistiques') loadStats(); if(tabId==='analytics'){loadAdvancedAnalytics();loadMLPredictions();} if(tabId==='audit') loadAuditLogs(); }); }); }
        loadAllData();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
