@app.route('/api/stats/global', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_global_stats():
    try:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        stats = {
            'total': query.count(),
            'en_attente': query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count(),
            'traite': query.filter_by(statut='traite').count(),
            'urgent': query.filter_by(statut='urgent').count(),
            'archive': query.filter_by(statut='archive').count(),
            'haute_priorite': query.filter_by(priorite='high').count()
        }
        
        evolution = [
            {'mois': 'Jan 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Fev 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Mar 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Avr 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)},
            {'mois': 'Mai 2026', 'total': random.randint(5, 20)}
        ]
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'stats': stats,
            'evolution': evolution,
            'top_expediteurs': [],
            'par_service': {},
            'performance': [random.randint(60, 95) for _ in range(6)]
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/analytics/advanced', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_advanced_analytics():
    try:
        hourly_distribution = [{'heure': h, 'total': random.randint(0, 15)} for h in range(8, 20)]
        jours = ['Lundi', 'Mardi', 'Mercredi', 'Jeudi', 'Vendredi', 'Samedi', 'Dimanche']
        weekly_distribution = [{'jour': j, 'total': random.randint(5, 25)} for j in jours]
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'hourly_distribution': hourly_distribution,
            'weekly_distribution': weekly_distribution,
            'processing_time': [
                {'priorite': 'high', 'priorite_label': 'Haute', 'temps_moyen': random.randint(2, 8)},
                {'priorite': 'medium', 'priorite_label': 'Moyenne', 'temps_moyen': random.randint(8, 24)},
                {'priorite': 'low', 'priorite_label': 'Basse', 'temps_moyen': random.randint(24, 72)}
            ]
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/ml/predictions', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_ml_predictions():
    try:
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        en_attente = query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count()
        urgents = query.filter_by(priorite='high').filter(Courrier.statut != 'traite').count()
        
        predictions = [max(1, 5 + random.randint(-2, 5) + int(i * 0.1)) for i in range(30)]
        
        suggestions = []
        if en_attente > 10:
            suggestions.append(f"⚠️ ALERTE: {en_attente} courriers en attente de traitement")
        elif en_attente > 5:
            suggestions.append(f"📋 Attention: {en_attente} courriers en attente")
        else:
            suggestions.append("✅ Bon rythme: Peu de courriers en attente")
        
        if urgents > 3:
            suggestions.append(f"🚨 {urgents} courriers urgents nécessitent une attention particulière")
        
        suggestions.append("💡 Conseil: Traitez les courriers par ordre de priorité")
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'predictions_30j': predictions,
            'jours_labels': [(datetime.now() + timedelta(days=i)).strftime('%d/%m') for i in range(30)],
            'suggestions': suggestions,
            'volume_estime': sum(predictions),
            'en_attente': en_attente,
            'urgents': urgents
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES AUDIT ====================
@app.route('/api/audit/logs', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_audit_logs():
    try:
        page = int(request.args.get('page', 1))
        per_page = int(request.args.get('per_page', 50))
        
        logs = AuditLog.query.order_by(AuditLog.created_at.desc()).paginate(page=page, per_page=per_page)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'logs': [log.to_dict() for log in logs.items],
            'total': logs.total,
            'page': page,
            'pages': logs.pages
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES ASSIGNATION DES COURRIERS ====================
@app.route('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(8)
def get_pending_courriers_for_assignation():
    """Récupère les courriers en attente et urgents pour assignation"""
    try:
        # Récupérer tous les courriers en attente ou urgents
        query = Courrier.query.filter(
            Courrier.statut.in_(['en_attente', 'urgent'])
        ).order_by(Courrier.priorite.desc(), Courrier.created_at.asc())
        
        # Exclure les courriers déjà assignés et non traités
        assigned_ids = db.session.query(Assignation.courrier_id).filter(
            Assignation.status.in_(['pending', 'accepted'])
        ).subquery()
        query = query.filter(~Courrier.id.in_(assigned_ids))
        
        courriers = query.all()
        
        # Récupérer tous les utilisateurs par service
        users_by_service = db.session.query(
            User.id, User.username, User.nom_complet, User.service, Role.nom.label('role')
        ).join(Role).filter(User.statut == 'actif', User.role_id < 5).all()
        
        # Organiser les utilisateurs par service
        services_users = {}
        for u in users_by_service:
            service = u.service or 'Autre'
            if service not in services_users:
                services_users[service] = []
            services_users[service].append({
                'id': u.id,
                'username': u.username,
                'nom_complet': u.nom_complet,
                'role': u.role
            })
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'courriers': [c.to_dict() for c in courriers],
            'services_users': services_users,
            'total_pending': len(courriers)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/assign', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(8)
def assign_courrier():
    """Assigner un courrier à un utilisateur"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        courrier_id = data.get('courrier_id')
        assigned_to_user_id = data.get('assigned_to_user_id')
        commentaire = data.get('commentaire', '')
        
        if not courrier_id or not assigned_to_user_id:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Données manquantes'})
        
        courrier = Courrier.query.get(courrier_id)
        if not courrier:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Courrier non trouvé'})
        
        assigned_to = User.query.get(assigned_to_user_id)
        if not assigned_to:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Utilisateur non trouvé'})
        
        # Vérifier si déjà assigné
        existing = Assignation.query.filter_by(
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            status='pending'
        ).first()
        
        if existing:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Courrier déjà assigné'})
        
        # Créer l'assignation
        assignation = Assignation(
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            assigned_to_user_id=assigned_to_user_id,
            assigned_by_user_id=session['user_id'],
            service_destinataire=assigned_to.service,
            commentaire=commentaire,
            status='pending'
        )
        
        db.session.add(assignation)
        
        # Créer notification
        notification = NotificationAssignation(
            user_id=assigned_to_user_id,
            courrier_id=courrier_id,
            message=f"Nouveau courrier assigné: {courrier.objet[:100]}",
            type='assignation'
        )
        
        db.session.add(notification)
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('assign', 'courrier', courrier_id, f"Assigné à {assigned_to.username}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': f'Courrier assigné à {assigned_to.nom_complet}'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/my-courriers', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_my_assigned_courriers():
    """Récupère les courriers assignés à l'utilisateur connecté"""
    try:
        user_id = session['user_id']
        
        assignations = Assignation.query.filter_by(
            assigned_to_user_id=user_id
        ).filter(Assignation.status.in_(['pending', 'accepted'])).all()
        
        courrier_ids = [a.courrier_id for a in assignations]
        courriers = Courrier.query.filter(Courrier.id.in_(courrier_ids)).all()
        
        # Ajouter les infos d'assignation
        result = []
        for c in courriers:
            assign = next((a for a in assignations if a.courrier_id == c.id), None)
            if assign:
                c_dict = c.to_dict()
                c_dict['assignation_id'] = assign.id
                c_dict['assignation_status'] = assign.status
                c_dict['assignation_commentaire'] = assign.commentaire
                assigned_by = User.query.get(assign.assigned_by_user_id)
                c_dict['assigned_by'] = assigned_by.nom_complet if assigned_by else None
                result.append(c_dict)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'courriers': result,
            'total': len(result)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/<int:assignation_id>/traiter', methods=['PUT'])
@permission_required(3)
def traiter_courrier_assigne(assignation_id):
    """Marquer un courrier assigné comme traité"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        commentaire_traitement = data.get('commentaire', '')
        
        assignation = Assignation.query.get_or_404(assignation_id)
        
        # Vérifier que l'utilisateur est bien celui assigné
        if assignation.assigned_to_user_id != session['user_id']:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non autorisé'})
        
        # Mettre à jour l'assignation
        assignation.status = 'completed'
        assignation.date_traitement = datetime.utcnow()
        if assignation.commentaire:
            assignation.commentaire = assignation.commentaire + f"\n\nTraitement: {commentaire_traitement}"
        else:
            assignation.commentaire = f"Traitement: {commentaire_traitement}"
        
        # Mettre à jour le statut du courrier
        courrier = Courrier.query.get(assignation.courrier_id)
        courrier.statut = 'traite'
        courrier.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
        
        # Créer notification pour le directeur
        notification = NotificationAssignation(
            user_id=assignation.assigned_by_user_id,
            courrier_id=assignation.courrier_id,
            message=f"Courrier traité par {User.query.get(session['user_id']).nom_complet}",
            type='completed'
        )
        db.session.add(notification)
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        log_action('traiter', 'courrier', assignation.courrier_id, f"Courrier traité par utilisateur #{session['user_id']}")
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Courrier marqué comme traité'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/notifications', methods=['GET'])
@permission_required(1)
def get_assignation_notifications():
    """Récupère les notifications non lues"""
    try:
        notifications = NotificationAssignation.query.filter_by(
            user_id=session['user_id'],
            is_read=False
        ).order_by(NotificationAssignation.created_at.desc()).limit(20).all()
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'notifications': [{
                'id': n.id,
                'courrier_id': n.courrier_id,
                'message': n.message,
                'type': n.type,
                'is_read': n.is_read,
                'created_at': n.created_at.strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M') if n.created_at else None
            } for n in notifications],
            'total': len(notifications)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/api/assignations/notifications/<int:notif_id>/read', methods=['PUT'])
@permission_required(1)
def mark_notification_read(notif_id):
    """Marquer une notification comme lue"""
    try:
        notification = NotificationAssignation.query.get_or_404(notif_id)
        if notification.user_id != session['user_id']:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Non autorisé'})
        
        notification.is_read = True
        db.session.commit()
        
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


# ==================== ROUTES CHATBOT ====================
@app.route('/api/chatbot/message', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(1)
def chatbot_message():
    data = request.get_json()
    user_message = data.get('message', '').lower()
    
    total = 0
    en_attente = 0
    user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
    if user:
        query = Courrier.query
        if user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        total = query.count()
        en_attente = query.filter_by(statut='en_attente').count()
    
    responses = {
        'courrier': f"Vous avez actuellement {total} courriers au total.",
        'assignation': "Les assignations permettent au directeur de répartir les courriers entre les services.",
        'urgent': f"Il y a {en_attente} courriers en attente de traitement."
    }
    
    for keyword, response in responses.items():
        if keyword in user_message:
            return jsonify({'success': True, 'response': response})
    
    return jsonify({
        'success': True,
        'response': f"📊 {total} courriers, {en_attente} en attente.\n\nPosez-moi une question sur: courriers, assignation, urgent"
    })


# ==================== ROUTES EXPORT ====================
@app.route('/api/export/pdf', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(5)
def export_to_pdf():
    from fpdf import FPDF
    
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        courrier_ids = data.get('courrier_ids', [])
        
        user = User.query.get(session['user_id'])
        query = Courrier.query
        
        if courrier_ids:
            query = query.filter(Courrier.id.in_(courrier_ids))
        elif user.role.niveau < 8:
            query = query.filter_by(service_destinataire=user.service)
        
        courriers = query.limit(100).all()
        
        pdf = FPDF()
        pdf.add_page()
        pdf.set_font('Arial', 'B', 14)
        pdf.cell(0, 10, "Rapport des courriers", 0, 1, 'C')
        pdf.ln(10)
        
        pdf.set_font('Arial', 'B', 10)
        pdf.cell(20, 10, "ID", 1)
        pdf.cell(60, 10, "Expediteur", 1)
        pdf.cell(60, 10, "Objet", 1)
        pdf.cell(30, 10, "Date", 1)
        pdf.ln()
        
        pdf.set_font('Arial', '', 9)
        for c in courriers:
            pdf.cell(20, 8, str(c.id), 1)
            pdf.cell(60, 8, c.expediteur[:25], 1)
            pdf.cell(60, 8, c.objet[:25], 1)
            pdf.cell(30, 8, c.date_reception.strftime('%d/%m/%Y'), 1)
            pdf.ln()
        
        filename = f"rapport_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.pdf"
        filepath = os.path.join(Config.ARCHIVE_FOLDER, filename)
        pdf.output(filepath)
        
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'pdf_url': f'/download/{filename}'})
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})


@app.route('/download/<filename>')
def download_file(filename):
    return send_file(os.path.join(Config.ARCHIVE_FOLDER, filename), as_attachment=True)


@app.route('/api/archive/auto', methods=['POST'])
@permission_required(8)
def auto_archive():
    try:
        archive_date = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=30)
        to_archive = Courrier.query.filter(
            Courrier.statut == 'traite',
            Courrier.created_at < archive_date
        ).all()
        
        for courrier in to_archive:
            courrier.statut = 'archive'
        
        db.session.commit()
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'message': f"{len(to_archive)} courriers archivés",
            'archived_count': len(to_archive)
        })
    
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)})

    <!-- MODALS -->
    <div id="scanModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h2>📸 Scanner un courrier</h2><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeScanModal()">&times;</span></div><div class="upload-area" id="uploadArea"><div class="upload-icon">📁</div><p>Déposez l'image du courrier</p><input type="file" id="fileInput" style="display:none" accept="image/*,application/pdf" onchange="handleFileUpload(this.files[0])"></div><div id="previewContainer" style="display:none;"><img id="previewImage" class="preview-image"></div><div id="scanProgress" style="display:none;"><div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" id="scanProgressFill"></div></div><p>Analyse OCR en cours...</p></div><div id="scanResult" style="display:none;"><div id="ocrConfidence"></div><div id="aiClassification"></div><div id="aiSummary" class="ai-summary"></div><div><strong>📝 Texte extrait:</strong></div><div id="extractedText"></div><button class="btn-add" onclick="saveScannedCourrier()">💾 Enregistrer</button></div></div></div>

    <div id="editCourrierModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>✏️ Modifier le courrier</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeEditModal()">&times;</span></div><div id="editCourrierInfo"></div><div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">Objet</label><input type="text" id="editObjet" class="form-control"></div><div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">Statut</label><select id="editStatut" class="form-control"><option value="en_attente">En attente</option><option value="traite">Traité</option><option value="urgent">Urgent</option><option value="archive">Archivé</option></select></div><div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">Priorité</label><select id="editPriorite" class="form-control"><option value="low">Basse</option><option value="medium">Moyenne</option><option value="high">Haute</option></select></div><div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">Contenu</label><textarea id="editContenu" class="form-control" rows="3"></textarea></div><div style="display: flex; gap: 10px;"><button class="btn-add" onclick="saveEditCourrier()" style="flex:1;">💾 Enregistrer</button><button class="btn-add" onclick="closeEditModal()" style="flex:1; background:#6c757d;">Annuler</button></div></div></div>
    <div id="multipleTraiterModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>✅ Traitement multiple</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeMultipleTraiterModal()">&times;</span></div><div id="selectedTraiterList"></div><textarea id="traiterMultipleComment" class="form-control" rows="3" placeholder="Rapport..."></textarea><button class="btn-add" onclick="confirmMultipleTraiter()">✅ Confirmer</button></div></div>
    <div id="notificationsModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>🔔 Notifications</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeNotificationsModal()">&times;</span></div><div id="notificationsList"></div></div></div>
    <div id="deleteConfirmModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>🗑️ Confirmer la suppression</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeDeleteConfirmModal()">&times;</span></div><p>Êtes-vous sûr de vouloir supprimer ce courrier ? Cette action est irréversible.</p><div><button class="btn-add btn-danger" onclick="confirmDeleteCourrier()">Supprimer</button><button class="btn-add" onclick="closeDeleteConfirmModal()">Annuler</button></div></div></div>
    <div id="archiveConfirmModal" class="modal"><div class="modal-content"><div class="modal-header"><h3>📦 Confirmer l'archivage</h3><span class="close-modal" onclick="closeArchiveConfirmModal()">&times;</span></div><p>Êtes-vous sûr de vouloir archiver ce courrier ?</p><div><button class="btn-add" style="background:#06b6d4;" onclick="confirmArchiveCourrier()">📦 Archiver</button><button class="btn-add" onclick="closeArchiveConfirmModal()">Annuler</button></div></div></div>

    <script>
        // ==================== VARIABLES ====================
        let currentPage = 1, totalPages = 1;
        let currentPageTraitement = 1, totalPagesTraitement = 1;
        let traitementCourriersFiltres = [], selectedTraitementIds = [];
        let charts = {}, userRole = '', userNiveau = 0, userPermissions = {};
        let allCourriers = [], pendingCourriers = [], servicesUsers = {};
        let currentAssignCourrier = null, currentTraiterAssignation = null;
        let currentScanFile = null, currentAnalysis = null;
        let currentPdfUrl = '';
        let courrierToDelete = null;
        let courrierToArchive = null;
        let courrierToEdit = null;

        // ==================== GESTION DES PERMISSIONS UI ====================
        function updateUIByPermissions(niveau, role) {
            userNiveau = niveau;
            userRole = role;
            const canScan = niveau >= 3;
            const canExport = niveau >= 5;
            const canAssign = niveau >= 8;
            const canAudit = niveau >= 10;
            
            document.querySelectorAll('.assignation-only, .traitement-only, .stats-only, .analytics-only').forEach(el => {
                if (canAssign) el.classList.remove('hidden');
                else el.classList.add('hidden');
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('.audit-only').forEach(el => {
                if (canAudit) el.classList.remove('hidden');
                else el.classList.add('hidden');
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('.export-only').forEach(el => {
                if (canExport) el.classList.remove('hidden');
                else el.classList.add('hidden');
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('.scan-only').forEach(el => {
                if (canScan) el.classList.remove('hidden');
                else el.classList.add('hidden');
            });
            
            const roleMap = {1: 'Stagiaire', 3: 'Assistant', 5: 'Chargé d\'étude', 8: 'Chef de service', 10: 'Directeur'};
            document.getElementById('userRole').textContent = roleMap[niveau] || role;
        }

        // ==================== CHARGEMENT DES PERMISSIONS ====================
        async function loadUserPermissions() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/user/permissions', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    userPermissions = data.permissions;
                    userRole = data.role;
                    userNiveau = data.niveau;
                    updateUIByPermissions(userNiveau, userRole);
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            } catch(e) { return false; }
        }

        function toggleDarkMode() {
            document.body.classList.toggle('dark-mode');
            localStorage.setItem('darkMode', document.body.classList.contains('dark-mode'));
        }
        if (localStorage.getItem('darkMode') === 'true') document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');

        const uploadArea = document.getElementById('uploadArea');
        if(uploadArea) {
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.classList.add('drag-over'); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('dragleave', () => { uploadArea.classList.remove('drag-over'); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('drop', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); uploadArea.classList.remove('drag-over'); const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]; if(file) handleFileUpload(file); });
            uploadArea.addEventListener('click', () => document.getElementById('fileInput').click());
        }

        async function checkAuth() {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/check_auth', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if (!data.success) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; } return data;
            } catch(e) { window.location.href = '/'; return false; }
        }

        async function loadAllData() {
            const auth = await checkAuth();
            if (!auth) return;
            await loadUserPermissions();
            if (auth.user) {
                document.getElementById('username').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userName').textContent = auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username;
                document.getElementById('userAvatar').textContent = (auth.user.nom_complet || auth.user.username).charAt(0).toUpperCase();
            }
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
            await loadAdvancedAnalytics();
            await loadAuditLogs();
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            initCharts();
            setupTabs();
            if (userNiveau >= 8) await loadPendingCourriers();
            loadNotifications();
            setInterval(() => loadNotifications(), 30000);
        }

        // ==================== COURRIERS ====================
        function clearFilters() {
            document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dateStartFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dateEndFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('statusFilter').value = 'all';
            document.getElementById('priorityFilter').value = 'all';
            loadCourriers();
        }

        async function loadCourriers() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput')?.value || '';
            const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const priority = document.getElementById('priorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
            const dateStart = document.getElementById('dateStartFilter')?.value || '';
            const dateEnd = document.getElementById('dateEndFilter')?.value || '';
            
            let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPage}&per_page=10`;
            if(search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
            if(status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
            if(priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
            if(dateStart) url += `&date_start=${dateStart}`;
            if(dateEnd) url += `&date_end=${dateEnd}`;
            
            try {
                const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) {
                    allCourriers = data.courriers || [];
                    displayCourriersTable(allCourriers);
                    displayRecentCourriers(allCourriers.slice(0, 5).reverse());
                    updateStatsCards(allCourriers);
                    updateCharts(allCourriers);
                    totalPages = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfo').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
                    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = currentPage <= 1;
                    document.getElementById('nextBtn').disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
                    document.getElementById('courrierBadge').textContent = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
                    document.getElementById('traitementBadge').innerHTML = allCourriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        function displayCourriersTable(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('courriersTableBody');
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">📭 Aucun courrier</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            
            const canEdit = userNiveau >= 5;
            const canDelete = userNiveau >= 8;
            
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => {
                const hasScan = c.scan_image_path && c.scan_image_path !== null && c.scan_image_path !== '';
                const canArchive = c.statut !== 'archive';
                return `<tr style="${c.priorite === 'high' ? 'border-left: 3px solid #ef4444;' : ''}">
                    <td>${c.id}</span>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 50))}</span>
                    <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || c.classification_ia || 'normal')}</span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span><td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span>
                    <td>${c.ocr_confidence ? `🎯 ${c.ocr_confidence}%` : '-'}</span>
                    <td>
                        <button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button>
                        ${hasScan ? `<button class="action-btn btn-pdf" onclick="previewScanByCourrierId(${c.id})">📄</button>` : ''}
                        ${canEdit ? `<button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="openEditModal(${c.id})">✏️</button>` : ''}
                        ${canArchive && canDelete ? `<button class="action-btn btn-archive" onclick="openArchiveModal(${c.id})" title="Archiver">📦</button>` : ''}
                        ${canDelete ? `<button class="action-btn btn-delete" onclick="openDeleteModal(${c.id})">🗑️</button>` : ''}
                     </span>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        function displayRecentCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recentCourriers');
            if(!tbody) return;
            if(!courriers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">📭 Aucun courrier récent</span></span></td></span>'; return; }
            
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => {
                const hasScan = c.scan_image_path && c.scan_image_path !== null && c.scan_image_path !== '';
                const canEdit = userNiveau >= 5;
                return `<tr>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</span>
                    <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || 'normal')}</span>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span>
                    <td>${c.ocr_confidence ? `${c.ocr_confidence}%` : '-'}</span>
                    <td>
                        <button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrier(${c.id})">👁️</button>
                        ${hasScan ? `<button class="action-btn btn-pdf" onclick="previewScanByCourrierId(${c.id})">📄</button>` : ''}
                        ${canEdit ? `<button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="openEditModal(${c.id})">✏️</button>` : ''}
                     </span>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        function updateStatsCards(courriers) {
            const total = courriers.length, enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length;
            const traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length, urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent' || c.priorite === 'high').length;
            document.getElementById('statsGrid').innerHTML = `<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">📬 Total courriers</span><span class="stat-icon">📊</span></div><div class="stat-value">${total}</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">⏳ En attente</span><span class="stat-icon">⏰</span></div><div class="stat-value">${enAttente}</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">✅ Traités</span><span class="stat-icon">✓</span></div><div class="stat-value">${traites}</div></div>
                <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-header"><span class="stat-title">🚨 Urgents</span><span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span></div><div class="stat-value">${urgents}</div></div>`;
        }

        function getClassificationBadge(c) {
            const badges = { urgent:'badge-urgent', juridique:'badge-juridique', financier:'badge-financier', normal:'badge-normal' };
            const icons = { urgent:'🚨', juridique:'⚖️', financier:'💰', normal:'📋' };
            return `<span class="badge ${badges[c] || 'badge-normal'}">${icons[c] || '📋'} ${c || 'Normal'}</span>`;
        }

        function getPriorityBadge(p) { 
            if(p==='high') return '<span class="priority-high">🔴 Haute</span>';
            if(p==='medium') return '<span class="priority-medium">🟡 Moyenne</span>';
            return '<span class="priority-low">🟢 Basse</span>';
        }
        
        function getStatusBadge(s) { 
            const b = { en_attente:'badge-en_attente', traite:'badge-traite', urgent:'badge-urgent', archive:'badge-archive' };
            const i = { en_attente:'⏳', traite:'✅', urgent:'🚨', archive:'📦' };
            return `<span class="badge ${b[s]||'badge-en_attente'}">${i[s]||'📋'} ${s?.replace('_',' ')||'En attente'}</span>`;
        }

        // ==================== MODIFICATION ====================
        function openEditModal(courrierId) {
            if (userNiveau < 5) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante pour modifier', 'error'); return; }
            const courrier = allCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId);
            if (!courrier) return;
            courrierToEdit = courrierId;
            document.getElementById('editObjet').value = courrier.objet || '';
            document.getElementById('editStatut').value = courrier.statut || 'en_attente';
            document.getElementById('editPriorite').value = courrier.priorite || 'medium';
            document.getElementById('editContenu').value = courrier.contenu || '';
            document.getElementById('editCourrierModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function saveEditCourrier() {
            if (!courrierToEdit) return;
            const data = {
                objet: document.getElementById('editObjet').value,
                statut: document.getElementById('editStatut').value,
                priorite: document.getElementById('editPriorite').value,
                contenu: document.getElementById('editContenu').value
            };
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/courriers/${courrierToEdit}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    credentials: 'include',
                    body: JSON.stringify(data)
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    showNotification('Courrier modifié avec succès', 'success');
                    closeEditModal();
                    await loadCourriers();
                    await loadTraitementCourriers();
                    await loadStats();
                } else {
                    showNotification(result.message || 'Erreur lors de la modification', 'error');
                }
            } catch(e) {
                showNotification('Erreur lors de la modification', 'error');
            }
        }

        function closeEditModal() {
            document.getElementById('editCourrierModal').classList.remove('active');
            courrierToEdit = null;
        }

        // ==================== ARCHIVAGE ====================
        function openArchiveModal(courrierId) {
            if (userNiveau < 8) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante pour archiver', 'error'); return; }
            courrierToArchive = courrierId;
            document.getElementById('archiveConfirmModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        function closeArchiveConfirmModal() {
            document.getElementById('archiveConfirmModal').classList.remove('active');
            courrierToArchive = null;
        }

        async function confirmArchiveCourrier() {
            if (!courrierToArchive) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/courriers/${courrierToArchive}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    credentials: 'include',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ statut: 'archive' })
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showNotification('Courrier archivé avec succès', 'success');
                    closeArchiveConfirmModal();
                    await loadCourriers();
                    await loadTraitementCourriers();
                    await loadStats();
                } else {
                    showNotification(data.message || 'Erreur lors de l\'archivage', 'error');
                }
            } catch(e) {
                showNotification('Erreur lors de l\'archivage', 'error');
            }
        }

        // ==================== SUPPRESSION ====================
        function openDeleteModal(courrierId) {
            if (userNiveau < 8) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante', 'error'); return; }
            courrierToDelete = courrierId;
            document.getElementById('deleteConfirmModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        function closeDeleteConfirmModal() { document.getElementById('deleteConfirmModal').classList.remove('active'); courrierToDelete = null; }

        async function confirmDeleteCourrier() {
            if (!courrierToDelete) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/courriers/${courrierToDelete}`, { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showNotification('Courrier supprimé avec succès', 'success');
                    closeDeleteConfirmModal();
                    await loadCourriers();
                    await loadTraitementCourriers();
                    await loadStats();
                } else showNotification(data.message || 'Erreur', 'error');
            } catch(e) { showNotification('Erreur lors de la suppression', 'error'); }
        }

        // ==================== CHARTES ====================
        function initCharts() {
            const ctxStatus = document.getElementById('statusChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPriority = document.getElementById('priorityChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxMonthly = document.getElementById('monthlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxTop = document.getElementById('topExpediteursChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxHourly = document.getElementById('hourlyChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxML = document.getElementById('mlPredictionChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxClassif = document.getElementById('classificationChart')?.getContext('2d');
            const ctxPerf = document.getElementById('performanceChart')?.getContext('2d');
            if(ctxStatus) charts.statusChart = new Chart(ctxStatus, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['En attente','Traité','Urgent','Archivé'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#f59e0b','#10b981','#ef4444','#06b6d4'] }] } });
            if(ctxPriority) charts.priorityChart = new Chart(ctxPriority, { type:'pie', data:{ labels:['Haute','Moyenne','Basse'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ef4444','#f59e0b','#10b981'] }] } });
            if(ctxMonthly) charts.monthlyChart = new Chart(ctxMonthly, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:[], borderColor:'#7c3aed' }] } });
            if(ctxTop) charts.topExpediteursChart = new Chart(ctxTop, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ data:[], backgroundColor:'#a855f7' }] } });
            if(ctxHourly) charts.hourlyChart = new Chart(ctxHourly, { type:'bar', data:{ labels:Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>`${i}h`), datasets:[{ label:'Courriers', data:Array(24).fill(0), backgroundColor:'#7c3aed' }] } });
            if(ctxML) charts.mlChart = new Chart(ctxML, { type:'line', data:{ labels:[], datasets:[{ label:'Prédiction', data:[], borderColor:'#dc3545' }] } });
            if(ctxClassif) charts.classificationChart = new Chart(ctxClassif, { type:'doughnut', data:{ labels:['Urgent','Juridique','Financier','Normal'], datasets:[{ data:[0,0,0,0], backgroundColor:['#ef4444','#06b6d4','#10b981','#6c757d'] }] } });
            if(ctxPerf) charts.performanceChart = new Chart(ctxPerf, { type:'line', data:{ labels:['Jan','Fev','Mar','Avr','Mai','Juin'], datasets:[{ label:'Performance (%)', data:[0,0,0,0,0,0], borderColor:'#10b981' }] } });
        }

        function updateCharts(courriers) {
            const enAttente = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente').length, traites = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'urgent').length, archives = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'archive').length;
            const high = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length, medium = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'medium').length, low = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'low').length;
            const classif = { urgent:0, juridique:0, financier:0, normal:0 };
            courriers.forEach(c => { const cl = c.ai_classification || c.classification_ia || 'normal'; if(classif[cl] !== undefined) classif[cl]++; else classif.normal++; });
            if(charts.statusChart) { charts.statusChart.data.datasets[0].data = [enAttente, traites, urgents, archives]; charts.statusChart.update(); }
            if(charts.priorityChart) { charts.priorityChart.data.datasets[0].data = [high, medium, low]; charts.priorityChart.update(); }
            if(charts.classificationChart) { charts.classificationChart.data.datasets[0].data = [classif.urgent, classif.juridique, classif.financier, classif.normal]; charts.classificationChart.update(); }
        }

        async function loadStats() {
            if (userNiveau < 8) return;
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/stats/global', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.evolution && charts.monthlyChart) { charts.monthlyChart.data.labels = data.evolution.map(e => e.mois); charts.monthlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.evolution.map(e => e.total); charts.monthlyChart.update(); }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadAdvancedAnalytics() {
            if (userNiveau < 8) return;
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/analytics/advanced', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && data.hourly_distribution && charts.hourlyChart) { const hours = Array(24).fill(0); data.hourly_distribution.forEach(h => { if(h.heure>=0 && h.heure<24) hours[h.heure] = h.total; }); charts.hourlyChart.data.datasets[0].data = hours; charts.hourlyChart.update(); }
            await loadMLPredictions();
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        async function loadMLPredictions() {
            if (userNiveau < 8) return;
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/ml/predictions', { credentials: 'include' }); const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success && charts.mlChart) { charts.mlChart.data.labels = data.jours_labels; charts.mlChart.data.datasets[0].data = data.predictions_30j; charts.mlChart.update(); 
            document.getElementById('mlSuggestionsList').innerHTML = (data.suggestions || []).map(s => `<div class="ai-summary">💡 ${s}</div>`).join(''); } } catch(e) { console.error(e); } }

        // ==================== AUDIT TRAIL ====================
        async function loadAuditLogs() {
            if (userNiveau < 10) return;
            try { 
                const res = await fetch('/api/audit/logs', { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success && data.logs) { 
                    document.getElementById('auditTimeline').innerHTML = data.logs.slice(0,30).map(log => `<div class="timeline-item">
                        <div class="timeline-date">${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        <div class="timeline-content">
                            <span class="timeline-user">${escapeHtml(log.username)}</span> 
                            a effectué <strong>${log.action}</strong><br>
                            <small>${escapeHtml(log.details || '-')}</small>
                        </div>
                    </div>`).join(''); 
                    if(data.logs.length === 0) {
                        document.getElementById('auditTimeline').innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">📭 Aucun log d\'audit</p>';
                    }
                }
            } catch(e) { 
                console.error(e);
                document.getElementById('auditTimeline').innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:red;">❌ Erreur chargement des logs</p>';
            }
        }
        
        function refreshAuditLogs() { 
            showNotification('Rafraîchissement des logs...', 'info');
            loadAuditLogs(); 
        }

        // ==================== TRAITEMENT ====================
        async function loadTraitementCourriers() {
            if (userNiveau < 8) return;
            try {
                let url = `/api/courriers?page=${currentPageTraitement}&per_page=10`;
                const search = document.getElementById('traitementSearchInput')?.value || '';
                const status = document.getElementById('traitementStatusFilter')?.value || 'all';
                const priority = document.getElementById('traitementPriorityFilter')?.value || 'all';
                const classification = document.getElementById('traitementClassificationFilter')?.value || 'all';
                
                if (search) url += `&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;
                if (status !== 'all') url += `&status=${status}`;
                if (priority !== 'all') url += `&priority=${priority}`;
                if (classification !== 'all') url += `&classification=${classification}`;
                
                const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    traitementCourriersFiltres = data.courriers || [];
                    displayTraitementCourriers(traitementCourriersFiltres);
                    totalPagesTraitement = data.pages || 1;
                    document.getElementById('pageInfoTraitement').innerHTML = `Page ${currentPageTraitement} / ${totalPagesTraitement}`;
                    const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prevBtnTraitement');
                    const nextBtn = document.getElementById('nextBtnTraitement');
                    if (prevBtn) prevBtn.disabled = currentPageTraitement <= 1;
                    if (nextBtn) nextBtn.disabled = currentPageTraitement >= totalPagesTraitement;
                    updateTraitementStats(data.courriers || []);
                    
                    const filterInfo = document.getElementById('traitementFilterInfo');
                    if (filterInfo && (search || status !== 'all' || priority !== 'all' || classification !== 'all')) {
                        filterInfo.innerHTML = `🔍 ${data.courriers?.length || 0} résultat(s)`;
                    } else if (filterInfo) {
                        filterInfo.innerHTML = '';
                    }
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        function displayTraitementCourriers(courriers) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('traitementCourriersTable');
            if (!courriers.length) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">📭 Aucun courrier à traiter</span></span></td></span>';
                return;
            }
            
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => {
                const delai = calculerDelai(c.date_reception);
                const delaiClass = delai > 7 ? 'text-danger' : (delai > 3 ? 'text-warning' : 'text-success');
                const hasScan = c.scan_image_path && c.scan_image_path !== null && c.scan_image_path !== '';
                return `<tr style="${c.priorite === 'high' ? 'border-left: 3px solid #ef4444;' : ''}">
                    <td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="traitement-checkbox" value="${c.id}" ${c.statut === 'traite' ? 'disabled' : ''} onchange="updateTraitementSelectedCount()"></span>
                    <td><strong>#${c.id}</strong></span>
                    <td>${formatDate(c.date_reception)}</span>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></span>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0, 40))}</span>
                    <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || c.classification_ia || 'normal')}</span>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span>
                    <td>${getStatusBadge(c.statut)}</span>
                    <td class="${delaiClass}">${delai > 0 ? `${delai} jour(s)` : 'Aujourd\'hui'} ${delai > 7 ? ' ⚠️' : ''}</span>
                    <td>
                        <button class="action-btn btn-view" onclick="viewCourrierDetail(${c.id})">👁️</button>
                        ${hasScan ? `<button class="action-btn btn-pdf" onclick="previewScanByCourrierId(${c.id})">📄</button>` : ''}
                        <button class="action-btn btn-edit" onclick="quickTraiterCourrier(${c.id})" style="color:#10b981;">✅</button>
                     </span>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        function previewScanByCourrierId(courrierId) {
            if (!courrierId) { showNotification('ID de courrier invalide', 'error'); return; }
            showNotification('Chargement du document...', 'info');
            const url = `/api/scan/view/${courrierId}`;
            currentPdfUrl = url;
            fetch(url, { method: 'HEAD', credentials: 'include' })
                .then(response => {
                    if (response.ok) { document.getElementById('pdfPreviewIframe').src = url; document.getElementById('pdfPreviewModal').classList.add('active'); }
                    else if (response.status === 404) showNotification('Document non trouvé', 'error');
                    else showNotification('Erreur lors du chargement', 'error');
                })
                .catch(() => showNotification('Impossible de charger le document', 'error'));
        }

        function calculerDelai(dateReception) {
            if (!dateReception) return 0;
            const reception = new Date(dateReception);
            const aujourdhui = new Date();
            const diffTime = Math.abs(aujourdhui - reception);
            return Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        }

        function formatDate(dateString) {
            if (!dateString) return '-';
            const date = new Date(dateString);
            return date.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR');
        }

        function updateTraitementStats(courriers) {
            const aTraiter = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'en_attente' || c.statut === 'urgent').length;
            const urgents = courriers.filter(c => c.priorite === 'high').length;
            const aujourdhui = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            const traitesAujourdhui = courriers.filter(c => c.statut === 'traite' && c.date_traitement === aujourdhui).length;
            let totalDelai = 0, count = 0;
            courriers.forEach(c => { if (c.statut !== 'traite' && c.date_reception) { totalDelai += calculerDelai(c.date_reception); count++; } });
            const delaiMoyen = count > 0 ? (totalDelai / count).toFixed(1) : 0;
            document.getElementById('totalATraiter').textContent = aTraiter;
            document.getElementById('urgentsCount').textContent = urgents;
            document.getElementById('traitesAujourdhui').textContent = traitesAujourdhui;
            document.getElementById('delaiMoyen').textContent = delaiMoyen;
        }

        function updateTraitementSelectedCount() {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:checked:not([disabled])');
            selectedTraitementIds = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
            document.getElementById('traitementSelectedInfo').innerHTML = `✅ ${selectedTraitementIds.length} sélectionné(s)`;
            document.getElementById('btnTraiterSelection').disabled = selectedTraitementIds.length === 0;
            const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox');
            if (selectAll) {
                const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
                selectAll.checked = checkboxes.length === allCheckboxes.length && allCheckboxes.length > 0;
            }
        }

        function toggleSelectAllTraitement() {
            const allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])');
            const someChecked = Array.from(allCheckboxes).some(cb => cb.checked);
            allCheckboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = !someChecked);
            updateTraitementSelectedCount();
        }

        function toggleSelectAllFromCheckboxTraitement() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.traitement-checkbox:not([disabled])').forEach(cb => cb.checked = document.getElementById('selectAllTraitementCheckbox').checked);
            updateTraitementSelectedCount();
        }

        function filterTraitementCourriers() { currentPageTraitement = 1; loadTraitementCourriers(); }
        function clearTraitementFilters() {
            document.getElementById('traitementSearchInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('traitementStatusFilter').value = 'all';
            document.getElementById('traitementPriorityFilter').value = 'all';
            document.getElementById('traitementClassificationFilter').value = 'all';
            filterTraitementCourriers();
        }

        function openMultipleTraiterModal() {
            if (!selectedTraitementIds.length) { showNotification('Sélectionnez des courriers', 'error'); return; }
            document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.add('active');
        }

        async function confirmMultipleTraiter() {
            const comment = document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value;
            if (!comment) { showNotification('Ajoutez un rapport', 'error'); return; }
            let s=0,e=0;
            for(const id of selectedTraitementIds) {
                try{ const res=await fetch(`/api/courriers/${id}`,{method:'PUT',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},credentials:'include',body:JSON.stringify({statut:'traite',commentaire_traitement:comment,date_traitement:new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]})}); if((await res.json()).success) s++; else e++; } catch(err){ e++; }
            }
            showNotification(`✅ ${s} traités, ❌ ${e} erreurs`, s>0?'success':'error');
            closeMultipleTraiterModal();
            document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value = '';
            selectedTraitementIds = [];
            await loadTraitementCourriers();
            await loadCourriers();
            await loadStats();
        }

        function previousPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement > 1) { currentPageTraitement--; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }
        function nextPageTraitement() { if(currentPageTraitement < totalPagesTraitement) { currentPageTraitement++; loadTraitementCourriers(); } }

        // ==================== ASSIGNATION ====================
        async function loadPendingCourriers() {
            if (userNiveau < 8) return;
            try { 
                const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/courriers-pendings', { credentials: 'include' }); 
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.success) { 
                    pendingCourriers = data.courriers; 
                    servicesUsers = data.services_users; 
                    displayPendingCourriers(pendingCourriers); 
                } 
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); } 
        }
        
        function displayPendingCourriers(courriers) { 
            const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingCourriersTable');
            if(!courriers.length) { 
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">📭 Aucun courrier en attente d\'assignation</span></span></td></span>'; 
                return; 
            }
            tbody.innerHTML = courriers.map(c => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${c.id}</td>
                    <td>${c.date_reception || '-'}</td>
                    <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</strong></td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(c.objet?.substring(0,40))}</td>
                    <td>${getClassificationBadge(c.ai_classification || 'normal')}</td>
                    <td>${getPriorityBadge(c.priorite)}</span>
                    <td><button class="btn-add" onclick="openAssignModal(${c.id})" style="padding:5px 10px;">📨 Assigner</button></span>
                </tr>`).join('');
        }

        function openAssignModal(courrierId) { 
            const c = pendingCourriers.find(c => c.id === courrierId); 
            if(!c) return; 
            currentAssignCourrier = c; 
            document.getElementById('assignCourrierInfo').innerHTML = `<div><strong>Courrier #${c.id}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(c.objet)}<br>${escapeHtml(c.expediteur)}</div>`; 
            const select = document.getElementById('assignToUser'); 
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choisir --</option>'; 
            for(const [service, users] of Object.entries(servicesUsers)) { 
                users.forEach(u => { 
                    const opt = document.createElement('option'); 
                    opt.value = u.id; 
                    opt.textContent = `${u.nom_complet} (${u.role})`; 
                    select.appendChild(opt); 
                }); 
            } 
            document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active'); 
        }
        
        async function confirmAssign() { 
            const userId = document.getElementById('assignToUser').value; 
            if(!userId) { 
                showNotification('Choisissez un utilisateur','error'); 
                return; 
            } 
            try { 
                const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/assign',{
                    method:'POST',
                    headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
                    credentials:'include',
                    body:JSON.stringify({
                        courrier_id:currentAssignCourrier.id,
                        assigned_to_user_id:userId,
                        commentaire:document.getElementById('assignComment').value
                    })
                }); 
                const data=await res.json(); 
                if(data.success){
                    showNotification(data.message,'success');
                    closeAssignModal();
                    await loadPendingCourriers();
                    await loadCourriers();
                } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); 
            } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} 
        }

        // ==================== SCAN ====================
        function openScanModal() { if (userNiveau < 3) { showNotification('Permission insuffisante pour scanner', 'error'); return; } document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.add('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function closeScanModal() { document.getElementById('scanModal').classList.remove('active'); resetScanModal(); }
        function resetScanModal() { document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display='none'; document.getElementById('fileInput').value=''; currentScanFile=null; currentAnalysis=null; }
        function handleFileUpload(file) { if(!file) return; currentScanFile=file; const reader=new FileReader(); reader.onload=e=>{document.getElementById('previewImage').src=e.target.result;document.getElementById('previewContainer').style.display='block';}; reader.readAsDataURL(file); analyzeDocument(file); }
        async function analyzeDocument(file) { const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('document',file); document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='block'; let progress=0; const interval=setInterval(()=>{progress+=10;document.getElementById('scanProgressFill').style.width=progress+'%';if(progress>=100)clearInterval(interval);},150); try{ const res=await fetch('/api/scan/analyze',{method:'POST',credentials:'include',body:fd}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){ currentAnalysis=data; document.getElementById('extractedText').innerHTML=data.extracted_text; document.getElementById('ocrConfidence').innerHTML=`🎯 Confiance OCR: ${data.confidence}%`; document.getElementById('aiClassification').innerHTML=`🤖 Classification IA: ${data.ai_classification || 'Normal'}`; document.getElementById('aiSummary').innerHTML=`📝 Résumé: ${data.ai_summary || 'Courrier officiel'}`; document.getElementById('scanResult').style.display='block'; } else showNotification(data.message,'error'); } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur analyse','error');} finally{clearInterval(interval);document.getElementById('scanProgress').style.display='none';} }
        async function saveScannedCourrier() { if(!currentScanFile||!currentAnalysis){showNotification('Aucun document analysé','error');return;} const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('scan_image',currentScanFile); fd.append('courrier_data',JSON.stringify({date_reception:new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],expediteur:currentAnalysis.suggested_expediteur||'Document scanné',objet:currentAnalysis.suggested_objet||'Courrier scanné',contenu:currentAnalysis.extracted_text,priorite:currentAnalysis.suggested_priorite||'medium',ai_classification:currentAnalysis.ai_classification||'normal',ocr_confidence:currentAnalysis.confidence||0,ai_summary:currentAnalysis.ai_summary||''})); const res=await fetch('/api/scan/save',{method:'POST',credentials:'include',body:fd}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification('Courrier enregistré !','success');closeScanModal();loadCourriers();loadStats();} else showNotification(data.message,'error'); }

        // ==================== PDF & EXPORT ====================
        function previewPdf(url) { currentPdfUrl=url; document.getElementById('pdfPreviewIframe').src=url; document.getElementById('pdfPreviewModal').classList.add('active'); }
        function closePdfPreview() { document.getElementById('pdfPreviewModal').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('pdfPreviewIframe').src=''; }
        function downloadCurrentPdf() { window.open(currentPdfUrl,'_blank'); }

            document.getElementById('exportStatus').innerHTML='Génération...';

        async function archiveAuto() { try{ const res=await fetch('/api/archive/auto',{method:'POST',credentials:'include'}); const data=await res.json(); if(data.success){showNotification(data.message,'success');loadCourriers();} } catch(e){showNotification('Erreur','error');} }

        // ==================== UTILITAIRES ====================
        function viewCourrier(id) { const c = allCourriers.find(c=>c.id===id); if(c) alert(`📄 Courrier #${c.id}\nExpéditeur: ${c.expediteur}\nObjet: ${c.objet}\nDate: ${c.date_reception}\nPriorité: ${c.priorite}\nStatut: ${c.statut}\nContenu: ${c.contenu || 'Non renseigné'}`); }
        function viewCourrierDetail(id) { viewCourrier(id); }
        function editCourrier(id) { openEditModal(id); }
        function quickTraiterCourrier(id) { openTraiterModalWithData(id); }
        async function loadNotifications() { try { const res = await fetch('/api/assignations/notifications', { credentials:'include' }); const data = await res.json(); if(data.success) { const notifCount = document.getElementById('notifCount'); if(data.total>0){notifCount.textContent=data.total>9?'9+':data.total;notifCount.style.display='inline-block';} else notifCount.style.display='none'; } } catch(e){console.error(e);} }
        function showNotifications() { fetch('/api/assignations/notifications',{credentials:'include'}).then(r=>r.json()).then(data=>{ document.getElementById('notificationsList').innerHTML = data.success && data.notifications.length ? data.notifications.map(n=>`<div style="padding:10px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;"><strong>${n.type==='assignation'?'📨 Nouvelle assignation':'✅ Traitement terminé'}</strong><p>${n.message}</p><small>${n.created_at}</small></div>`).join('') : '<p>Aucune notification</p>'; document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.add('active'); }); }
        function escapeHtml(t) { if(!t) return ''; const div = document.createElement('div'); div.textContent = t; return div.innerHTML; }
        function showNotification(msg, type) { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = `notification ${type}`; n.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(n); setTimeout(()=>n.remove(),3000); }
        async function logout() { await fetch('/api/logout', { method:'POST', credentials:'include' }); window.location.href = '/'; }
        function previousPage() { if(currentPage>1){currentPage--;loadCourriers();} }
        function nextPage() { if(currentPage<totalPages){currentPage++;loadCourriers();} }
        function closeAssignModal() { document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('traiterModal').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('traiterComment').value = ''; }
        function closeNotificationsModal() { document.getElementById('notificationsModal').classList.remove('active'); }
        function closeMultipleTraiterModal() { document.getElementById('multipleTraiterModal').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('traiterMultipleComment').value = ''; }
        
        function setupTabs() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item=>{item.addEventListener('click',()=>{ const tabId=item.dataset.tab; document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('active')); item.classList.add('active'); document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab=>tab.classList.remove('active')); const targetTab = document.getElementById(`${tabId}Tab`); if(targetTab) targetTab.classList.add('active'); if(tabId==='courriers') loadCourriers(); if(tabId==='assignation' && userNiveau>=8) loadPendingCourriers(); if(tabId==='traitement' && userNiveau>=8) loadTraitementCourriers(); if(tabId==='statistiques' && userNiveau>=8) loadStats(); if(tabId==='analytics' && userNiveau>=8){loadAdvancedAnalytics();loadMLPredictions();} if(tabId==='audit' && userNiveau>=10) loadAuditLogs(); }); });
        }
        
        loadAllData();
    </script>
